# Walk playbook (walk lane)

How to make a machine's walk at 35 degrees, check it, and price it. Started 2026-11-05 by the walk lane ("Build mech
walk animations", Preston: "Get a walking animations session going that is skilled up from everything we learned").
Update this file when a lesson changes. The run log is `LOG.md` in this folder.

**Sources.** Read this session: `ANIMATION_PLAYBOOK.md` (all), `cam35_fleet/HANDOFF.md`, the rhino 35-degree walk
(`rhino_l/take-a/anim/walk35/`: REPORT.txt, clean/REPORT.txt, brief_run1.txt), the wolf walk4 run
(`wolf_m/take-b/anim/codex-walk4/REPORT.txt`, brief.txt, `common.txt`), the gorilla's set A walk report, the kits
(`docs/plan/moba-kits.md`), and the game feel lane's answers on speed (2026-11-05). General knowledge, not read
this session: the four key poses (Richard Williams, *The Animator's Survival Kit*), animal gait sequences
(Muybridge's plates, Hildebrand's gait studies), and the walk-to-run Froude rule (R. McNeill Alexander). Those
parts are marked as craft proposals. Nothing here is canon until Preston says so.

## 1. Rules that bind every walk
- **Build from the newest views.** Before any frames, look for `<take>/views_cam35_touchup/` and its APPROVED.txt.
  The rhino walk was drawn from `views_cam35` after Preston kept the touch-up, and needed `walk35_touchup.py` to
  recolour 40 frames afterwards.
- **The walk and the stills share one angle.** A machine's walk is drawn at 35 degrees only once its 35-degree stills
  are approved. Mixed angles pop at every start and stop.
- **Above the legs, every frame is the still**, moved only by the bob. Then idle to walk cannot pop. The rhino's
  35-degree walk matched its still on 0% differing pixels above the legs.
- **Legs are the still's own pixels, translated, never rotated or resampled.** Rotation left specks that cost a
  78,180-token cleanup on the rhino. Where a leg leans, step its parts 1 px a row.
- **Joints are redrawn, not pasted.** Pasted parts that did not join caused the cutoffs Preston rejected ("Too many
  cutoff etc."). Every joint and every join with the body is drawn in the still's colours and bands.
- **Feet stay planted** (section 7), and the drawn stride is checked against the game's speed (section 8).
- **No curled tails.** Tails lift and swing but stay straight, with the still's thickness and plate count.
- **Palette, canvas, offsets and feet row are the machine's own.** Keep its `palette.json`; place stills on the 192
  canvas at (+16, 160 - feet row).
- **Judge at 4x with the game's sel-out, the approved still in column 1.** He rejects "barely acceptable".
- **Never write into another lane's folders or the approved views.** This lane writes to `walk/<machine>/` only.
- **The session worktree has no `docs/art/moba`.** Write files in the worktree and copy them over with Bash (the
  fleet lane's method). C: was full on 2026-11-05, so the scratchpad was not usable.

## 2. Key poses (craft, proposal)
A walk is a controlled fall caught by each landing foot. Per foot, four poses:

| Pose | What happens | What the body does |
|---|---|---|
| Contact | The swinging foot lands ahead; the other foot of that pair is at the back of its stance | Hold the still's height |
| Down | Weight drops onto the landing foot | The dip: the body sinks 1 px (L can take 2); the shoulders or hips over that foot lead |
| Passing | The swinging foot passes the planted one, at its highest lift | The body returns to still height |
| Up | The planted foot pushes off | Body at still height (rising over it would lift the head off the still; not used yet) |

- On our machines the body only translates by whole pixels; armour does not squash. The rhino's approved bob is a
  1 px dip on f0 and f4 (the frames a fore foot lands).
- Head and tail lag the body by one frame (section 5). The rhino's pose-guided front walk leaned the head toward the
  planted foot, and Preston liked that walk.

## 3. Gaits by body (proposals unless marked approved)
| Machine | Gait | Phase per 8 frames | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| rhino (L) | four-beat lateral walk | LF swings f1-f3, LH f3-f5, RF f5-f7, RH f7-f1; one foot up on even frames, two on odd; lifts 2, 3, 2 | approved, installed at 35 |
| gorilla (L) | knuckle walk, diagonal pairs | right fist + left foot planted f0-f3, the other pair f4-f7; the fists swing as whole forearms | the set A walk (old angle) used diagonal pairs; the kit says "knuckle walking". Real gorillas use a four-beat walk: both are on the guide for Preston |
| wolf (M) | trot, diagonal pairs | pair A (near fore + far hind) planted f0-f3, pair B f4-f7; lifts 2, 4, 4, 2; P 5 at 10 fps, dips f1 and f5 (walk_tools gait trot4) | approved at the old angle |
| fennec (S) | trot | 10 fps, STRIDE 0.56 m/s in `fennec_kit.gd` | approved at the old angle; the walk is exempt from f0 = still (seated still) |
| hippo (L), pangolin (M) | four-beat walk, like the rhino | as rhino | proposal |
| chameleon (M) | slow diagonal-pair walk with a side-to-side rock | as wolf, longer holds | proposal |
| kangaroo (M) | hop: both hind feet together, tail and gloves trail | take-off f0-f1, air f2-f5, land f6-f7 | proposal; kit: "big hops" |
| squirrel (S) | hop or bound | as kangaroo, shorter air | proposal; kit: "hops and sudden changes of direction" |
| shoebill (M) | two legs: L planted f0-f3, R f4-f7, a body sway toward the planted leg | 8 frames | proposal |
| hummingbird (S) | no walk: hover drift (wing cycle plus a lean into the move) | wing cycle | confirmed: Preston took hover (2026-11-05, via the Hummingbird session); not in the walk queue. Kit: docs/plan/moba-kits.md |
| falcon (M) | check the kit and game feel (walk on talons, or a low glide) | | open |

## 4. Weight and timing by size (proposal; the rhino, wolf and fennec numbers are real)
| Size | Frames | ms a frame | Cycle | Dip | Lifts |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L | 8 | 125 (8 fps), rhino | 1.0 s | 1 px (2 px on a heavy landing, such as the gorilla dropping onto its fists) | 2-3 px |
| M | 8 | 100 (10 fps), wolf | 0.8 s | 1 px | 2-4 px |
| S | 8 | 100 (10 fps), fennec | 0.8 s | 0-1 px | 3-4 px |

Heavier means longer holds on the planted feet, a lower down pose and smaller lifts. Lighter means more air and a
quicker cycle. A cycle length is a placeholder until game feel and Preston set the speed (section 8).

## 5. Overlap and secondary motion
- **Tails:** straight. They trail the body bob by one frame and swing 1-2 px sideways, opposite the hips.
- **Ears, fins, antennae and cables:** trail by one frame, 1 px. Rigid armour plates move with their body part and
  never flex.
- **Gems, lenses and eyes:** carried with their part by translation, never redrawn smaller. Check them on every frame
  (the gorilla's hex gems broke in its hex_shield ne frames).
- **Arms that are legs** (gorilla fists, kangaroo gloves): the whole forearm block moves, and the shoulder join is
  redrawn.

## 6. Loops, frames and views
- **Seamless loop:** f7 to f0 is a normal step. Each foot does exactly one swing per cycle, the planted step is the
  same across the wrap, and the dip pattern repeats. Check it with an onion skin of f7 under f0.
- **Frames:** 8 a cycle, equal timing. Walks are retimed only with Preston's word (the wolf front and back were
  retimed into pairs on f0 and f4).
- **Views:** draw s, se, e, ne and n. The engine mirrors sw, w and nw, and a turn keeps the frame index.
- **Mirror phase (in the game since 2026-11-05, Preston: "Yes, fix it"):** a mirror swaps left and right, so in sw,
  w and nw the foot that is up is the other one. `show_facing()` in `scripts/moba3d/directional_sprite_3d.gd` now
  moves a walking machine's frame to (i + 4) mod 8 (minions (i + 2) mod 4) when it flips into or out of a mirrored
  view, the e-to-w snap included; idle and actions are untouched. Proof: `tools/debug/walk_turn_check.gd`, gorilla
  foot swaps 3 to 0. So every alternating walk (four-beat, diagonal, biped) must keep its two halves symmetric: the
  foot that swings on f(i) swings on its partner's side on f(i + 4), and the dip lands on f0 and f4. A hop has no
  left-right half, so check how a hop looks across that half-loop jump before drawing one. `walk_tools.py mirror`
  checks a gait.

## 7. Foot lock at 35 degrees
- A ground step of d texels along the body shows on screen as d x f x u. At pitch p and view yaw a (e 0, se 45,
  s 90), the screen vector is (cos a, sin a x sin p). At 35: e u (1, 0), f 1.000; se u (0.867, 0.497), f 0.815;
  s u (0, 1), f 0.574; ne and n the same with y negated.
- **Planted step a frame on the canvas = -P x f x u**, where P is the side-view step in px a frame. At P = 3:
  e (-3, 0); se (-2.12, -1.22); ne (-2.12, +1.22); s (0, -1.72); n (0, +1.72).
- **Lock rule:** the k-th planted frame's sole centre minus k planted steps stays within 2 px of the first. The rhino
  passed with e 0.00, se and ne 0.88, s and n 0.88 px.
- **Depth travel shows now.** At 35, a fore-and-back step in s and n shows at 0.574 of its length (0.16 at the old
  angle), so front and back walks need real foot travel, not reordered frames.
- **Toes toward the camera** may draw 2-3 px under the feet row (rhino s and n 163, se and ne 162). The floor hides
  none of it, and walk and idle both end on row 161 with sel-out.
- **The game camera is 30 degrees and the art is drawn at 35** (game feel, 2026-11-05). An s or n planted step drawn
  for 35 plays about 13% long in game: 0.22 px a frame at P = 3. That is under the 2 px lock gate; logged for game
  feel.

## 8. Stride against speed
- Drawn ground speed = P x fps x texel (m). Texel: L 0.020, M 0.018, S 0.016 m.
- Rhino: 3 x 8 x 0.020 = 0.48 m/s, kept by Preston ("Keep 0.48 m/s").
- The game: `move_speed = 3.5` m/s for every machine, a placeholder (`machine_test_player.gd:7`). `stride_speed`
  is one export for all machines (0.48), and `full_speed_walk_rate` is still 1.0, so the feet slide today.
- To plant at 3.5 m/s, a walk drawn at 0.48 m/s plays 7.3 times fast, or the side step must be 21.9 px a frame at
  L and 8 fps (24.3 at M, 27.3 at S). That is a run, not a walk: the gorilla's whole side view is 79 px long.
- **Natural walk speed (Froude, proposal):** v = sqrt(Fr x 9.81 x leg height in m). A comfortable walk is about
  Fr 0.25; animals switch to a run near Fr 0.5. `walk_tools.py stride` prints this per machine from its leg height.
- **Preston picks**, per game feel: lower move_speed, play the walk fast, draw longer strides, or add a run cycle.
  This lane never picks a speed. `stride_speed` needs a per-machine value once a second machine walks.

## 9. The checks on every walk (`walk_tools.py footlock`, plus the eye)
| Check | Pass |
|---|---|
| Foot lock per visible foot, from the frame pixels | spread 2 px or under in x and y |
| Swing feet | lift matches the gait sheet within 1 px; the swing goes forward along u |
| Above the legs | 0% differing pixels against the still after the dip |
| Lowest row per frame | the feet row, or 2-3 rows under it for toes toward the camera, listed |
| Palette, alpha, holes, strays, canvas edge | the still's colours only, binary alpha, one connected machine, no edge contact |
| Seams (`walk_tools.py seams`) | no slit where a limb slid back from the pinned body (solid in the idle, now an enclosed hole). Codex left them on the gorilla in e, se and ne; one `--fix` pass filled them with the body bands. Look first: open space beside a far limb at the silhouette edge is real and stays open, so never run a second pass |
| Loop | f7 to f0 is a normal step (planted step within 1 px of the others) |
| Mirror phase | the same physical foot up across s/sw and n/nw at each frame, or the offset noted |
| Stride | drawn m/s, and the playback rate needed at the current move_speed, reported |
| Eye | board at 4x with sel-out, still in column 1; a dot-grid review video (`make_walk35_review.py`) |
| Game | packed by the animation lane, then game feel's turn and speed test |

The foot-lock tool finds feet by the bottom of each column, so a foot hidden behind the body cannot be measured from a
composite. Codex's own verify measured those from its limb layers; ask for them in the brief.

## 10. Tools (stock-take 2026-11-05)
| Tool | Free or paid | Use here |
|---|---|---|

| Codex token cap (`rhino_l/take-a/anim/walk35/codex_cap/run_codex_capped_d.sh`, codex_tokens.py; same arguments as the old `run_codex_capped.sh`, but runs Codex from `D:/dev/_scratch/codex-cwd`, since hollowtide is moving to D:; animation lane, 2026-11-05) | free | kills a Codex run at a token cap; the watcher checks every 20 s, so a run can end about 3k over its cap |
| Aseprite MCP: render_onion_skin, compare_frames, audit_animation, tweens, Lua | free (local) | onion skins for the loop and the passing pose, frame diffs; needs an .aseprite file |

| Godot 4.7.2 headless check and Movie Maker | free | the in-game check and phone video, run by the lanes that own the game |
| Codex walk run (5 views) | paid: Codex tokens on Preston's plan | redraws the joints; the rhino took 274,960 tokens |
| PixelLab animate_with_skeleton_v3 | paid (inside the free daily quota of 20 generations, 3 a view) | fallback for straight s and n walks; held the rhino, lost the fennec |
| Retro Diffusion walking | paid, $0.14 a run | its side and se walks read as walks; ask before each run |
| SpriteCook animate | paid credits | not tried on a machine yet |

## 11. A walk, step by step
1. Confirm the 35-degree stills are approved, and find the newest folder (touch-up first).
2. `walk_tools.py stride` with the machine's leg height and size; agree P and fps with game feel.
3. `walk_tools.py gait` writes `targets.json`, the guides and the plan video for Preston. Free.
4. Price the Codex run (section 12) and wait for Preston's yes in his own words.
5. Codex run 1 (e, se, ne), then run 2 (s, n), each under a token cap, with the rhino's brief_run1 rules: translate,
   never rotate; joints by hand; body pinned.
6. `walk_tools.py footlock` and `seams` on every view, then the board at 4x with sel-out and a 5x zoom of each
   moving joint. The gorilla's cut-off ne limb passed every number and showed only in the zoom. Add to every brief:
   "where a limb slides back, draw the body behind it; no slit may show the background inside the body".
7. Send the frames to the touch-up lane for cleanup and sway. Once Preston approves its version, the walk lane packs
   them itself with pack_<machine>_3d.py and a backup (the animation lane was closed 2026-11-05). Game feel gets the
   turn and speed test. Message the hub at each handoff.

## 12. Pricing a walk
| Run | Rhino tokens | Expected with the lessons built in |
|---|---|---|
| Run 1: e, se, ne | 102,921 | 95k-115k |
| Leg cleanup | 78,180 (caused by rotation) | 0 if run 1 translates; 60k-80k if not |
| Run 2: s, n | 93,859 | 85k-105k |
| Repair pass for cutoffs (fennec) | 45k-190k for two sets | only if the board shows cutoffs |

Actual totals (Codex tokens, from each run's token-watch file):
| Machine | Run 1 (e, se, ne) | Run 2 (s, n, repairs) | Total |
|---|---|---|---|
| Gorilla (L, knuckle walk) | about 142k | 100,614 (with ne repair) | about 243k |
| Fennec (S, trot) | 134,714 | 106,214 (with two repairs) | 240,928, plus step A |
| Hippo (L, four-beat) | 224,713 (cap 160k, overshot one step) | 100,320 (cap 100k) | 325,033 |

## 13. Lessons from the gorilla, fennec and hippo (2026-11-05)
Body and look
- A body pinned to the still reads stiff. Preston on the fennec trot (0 px sway, 1 px dip): "isnt fluid". The fix,
  kept for all views ("Keep, do all views"), is touch-up's sway.py (touchup/fennec_s_walk/sway.py). It adds lean and
  bob after Codex, for free, by per-row offsets that fade to zero at the feet. Lean over the planted side: in a trot,
  over the landing front leg, 2 px; in a four-beat walk, away from the swinging side, 1 px for an L machine. Bob 1-2 px
  just after each landing, on top of the dip. Lock each planted foot by its own box, not the bottom rows: in the
  diagonals some feet stand 25 rows up (hippo se RH on row 135, ne RF on row 136).
- A new body pose (sit to stand, rear up) is a redraw, not a puppet. Preston rejected cut-and-reposition parts on the
  fennec death. Route: one image-tool sheet from the approved 35 still plus a pose reference (fennec step A,
  fennec_s/stand35), finished to the fixed palette. Scale a re-posed sheet by the head (ear span), not body length:
  scaled by length, the fennec's head came out 8% small.
- Front view (s): set the near legs wide enough apart, or the stance reads as sitting. Preston on the fennec: "Widen
  front view front legs a bit". Fixed by moving each leg out 2 px with the chest plate fixed (fennec_s/widen_s/).
Briefs
- Give a draw order per view: far legs, tail, body, near legs; in s, the jaw over the front legs as the still has it.
  Without one, the fennec's ne far hind leg covered its tail.
- Plates and discs belong to the body: where a limb uncovers one, draw it whole. Gate it with numbers: the piece count
  of the plate's colours equals the still's, with none of 4 px or fewer.
- The body stops at the belly: fill behind a moved leg only inside the body's outline. Hippo run 1 filled the still's
  leg area below the belly, which hung dark blocks under it and split a far foot. Run 2's belly gate fixed it: in each
  leg's column span, the body's lowest row stays within 1 px of the line between the columns on either side.
- Number gates work where "make it clean" does not (fennec: tail light px at least 63 in a box; gold pieces = 4).
  Add a speck gate: 1-2 px single-colour pieces in the leg rows, no more than the still plus 2.
- Have REPORT.txt updated after each view. Both hippo runs hit the cap during their own audits, so the walk lane ran
  their verify.py afterwards.
Checks
- walk_tools footlock's silhouette measure merges a sole with whatever touches it (close feet, the belly, the jaw).
  When that happens, Codex's stamp measure decides.
- The seams check finds holes, not extra fill. The hippo's hanging belly fill passed every number and showed only at
  8x, so always look at an 8x zoom of each leg area.
- A script fix needs the exact defect boxes. A straight belly-line guess removed the wrong pixels on the hippo
  (hippo_l/clean1/NOTE.txt). Hand cleanup in touch-up is the fallback.
Cost
- The capped runner reads tokens every 20 s, and one Codex step can add a whole context at once (hippo run 1 went
  from 149,939 to 224,713 in one tick). Quote Preston the worst case as the cap plus 75k.
In game
- Foot slide = speed / (stride_speed * speed_scale) (scripts/moba3d/machine_test_player.gd). A kit must set
  full_speed_walk_rate = walk_speed / STRIDE. The fennec kit does. ape_kit does not, so the gorilla slides 7.3x at
  move_speed 3.5. Check each machine's kit when packing.

Job, run 1: an 8-frame four-beat walk, 125 ms a frame (8 fps), looping, in these views: e, se, ne. The gait is the
rhino's four-beat walk, one foot after another: the left front foot (LF) swings f1-f3, the left hind (LH) f3-f5, the
right front (RF) f5-f7, the right hind (RH) f7, f0, f1 (wrapping). Each foot swings for 3 frames with lifts of 2, 3 and
2 px. The body, head and leg discs dip 1 px on f0 and f4. This is a heavy machine: short firm steps, feet flat when down,
no bounce beyond the 1 px dip. Each foot's path (offset along the body in side-view px, + = forward, and lift) is in
run1\targets.json "paths"; per view the path turns into screen px as offset * f * u, with the lift straight up:
  e: u = (1, 0), f = 1.0; a planted foot moves (-3.00, +0.00) px a frame on the canvas (x, y; y down)
  se: u = (0.8674, 0.4975), f = 0.8152; a planted foot moves (-2.12, -1.22) px a frame on the canvas
  ne: u = (0.8674, -0.4975), f = 0.8152; a planted foot moves (-2.12, +1.22) px a frame on the canvas
In se and ne the targets fall between pixels: round each frame's position so a planted foot's total travel stays within
0.5 px of the target. targets.json "views" gives the sole centre of every labelled foot in each still for every frame;
run1\guide_<view>.png shows them (the still faded, then a bar per foot per frame, filled when planted and outlined when
lifted, with a tick for the lift). R and L are the hippo's own right and left; its right side is the near side in e, se

Hidden in the stills: e LH and LF, se LH, ne LF; se LF only peeks out under the jaw. Draw a hidden foot only where the new
pose shows it; the jaw stays drawn over se's LF in every frame.

Method: no image tool, native pixels, as the fennec and gorilla walks did it. The body, head, jaw, tusks, eyes and armour
plates are the still, pixel for pixel, dipping 1 px on f0 and f4. Each leg, top to bottom, is: the big round violet hip or
shoulder disc, a steel knee joint, a short steel lower leg, and a wide foot pad (violet heel, steel toes, dark recess).
The hip and shoulder discs belong to the body: they stay whole and pinned to it and dip with it. The foot and lower leg
move together to the target, the knee joint moves about half as far, and the short leg between disc and knee is redrawn
as a leaning band in the still's own colours and shading bands, stepped 1 px per row or two, with its lit edge and dark
seam kept. On a lifted foot the lower leg rises with it and the foot stays flat (a hippo does not tip its toes). Every
part is the still's own pixels TRANSLATED, never rotated or resampled. Every joint and every join with the body is redrawn
by hand: no seams, no straight cut edges, no gaps, no part ending abruptly. Where a leg slides back, draw the body behind
it: no slit may show the background inside the body. Keep each leg's length within 1 px of the still's. The far legs
(darker in the still) are built the same way, one shade darker on their own ramp.

RF); in se the jaw is drawn over LF. Nothing of a far leg may sit on top of the body, head or a near leg.

where a leg covers it, draw the leg's edge cleanly over it. No loose specks.

"""hippo-walk35-run1. Native-part method reused from fennec/ape walks.
Only output beside this script. All input scripts/stills are read-only.

import sys

import json, ast, hashlib

Y,X=np.indices((192,192))
P={h:tuple(bytes.fromhex(h))+(255,) for h in '141218 211e27 26183f 3d3a44 4a347e 4e4b55 7258b4 8a8892 a990e0 c0bfc6 c4720e e4e4ea ffc23c fff1c4'.split()}
load=lambda p:np.array(Image.open(p).convert('RGBA'))
save=lambda a,p:Image.fromarray(a).save(R/p)
def rect(x0,y0,x1,y1):return (X>=x0)&(X<=x1)&(Y>=y0)&(Y<=y1)
# Contours are traced in the approved still. Discs never enter these masks.

# Full pinned disc masks, also used to prevent steel joins entering violet housings.
DISCS={
 'e':[[(52,130),(57,128),(63,128),(67,130),(69,134),(68,138),(65,141),(58,142),(53,139),(51,135)],[(94,128),(99,126),(104,127),(109,130),(111,136),(108,141),(104,144),(98,143),(93,140),(91,134)]],

def dark(a):
 out=a.copy()
 for h,j in [('a990e0','7258b4'),('7258b4','4a347e'),('4a347e','26183f'),('e4e4ea','c0bfc6'),('c0bfc6','8a8892'),('8a8892','4e4b55'),('4e4b55','3d3a44')]:out[np.all(a==P[h],axis=2)]=P[j]
 return out

def sources(v):
 ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');parts={};used=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
 discs=np.logical_or.reduce([b.mask(q) for q in DISCS[v]])&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
 for n,q in D[v].items():
  parts[n]={}
  for part in ['knee','lower']:
   mask=b.mask(q[part])&~discs&~used&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
   parts[n][part]=b.piece(ref,mask);used|=mask
 # Remove stationary fragments around the native foot contours, assigning them
 # to the complete lower block. Above the sole this includes its violet heel.
 extent={'e':{'RH':rect(47,148,78,161),'RF':rect(93,149,118,162)},
         'se':{'RH':rect(45,125,64,136),'RF':rect(71,149,101,162)},
         'ne':{'RH':rect(82,151,110,162),'RF':rect(116,128,138,138),'LH':rect(46,145,73,156)}}[v]
 for n,m in extent.items():
  extra=m&~used&~discs&(ref[:,:,3]>0);b.comp(parts[n]['lower'],b.piece(ref,extra));used|=extra
 # At the silhouettes the native heel meets the trunk without an alpha edge.
 # These row contours give the adjoining trunk pixels back to the pinned body.
 keepbody=np.zeros_like(used)
 if v=='se':
  keepbody|=rect(61,122,64,135)|rect(59,122,60,126)
 if v=='ne':
  keepbody|=rect(68,138,73,152)|rect(60,138,67,145)
  keepbody|=rect(116,124,120,130)
 for pp in parts.values():
  for part in pp:pp[part][keepbody]=0
 used[keepbody]=False
 if v=='se':
  # Full native front shin casing: the dark left rim moves with the shin.
  rim=rect(73,141,78,148)&~discs&(ref[:,:,3]>0)&~used
  b.comp(parts['RF']['lower'],b.piece(ref,rim));used|=rim
 if v=='e':
  rim=rect(51,144,57,147)&~discs&~used&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
  b.comp(parts['RH']['lower'],b.piece(ref,rim));used|=rim
 if v=='ne':
  for n,m in [('RF',rect(130,124,137,127)),('RH',rect(104,147,111,150))]:
   rim=m&~discs&~used&(ref[:,:,3]>0);b.comp(parts[n]['lower'],b.piece(ref,rim));used|=rim
 body=b.piece(ref,~used)
 # Hidden limbs are complete dark-ramp native joints, never cut-off peeks.
 hidden={'e':[('LH','RH',7,-7),('LF','RF',7,-8)],'se':[('LH','RH',44,-3),('LF','RH',76,22)],'ne':[('LF','RF',-36,-6)]}[v]
 for n,src,dx,dy in hidden:
  q=dict(D[v][src]);q.update({key:tuple(np.array(q[key])+[dx,dy]) for key in ['root','k','join']});q['hidden']=True;D[v][n]=q
  parts[n]={p:dark(b.shift(a,dx,dy)) for p,a in parts[src].items()}
 if v=='se':
  # Trace the native exposed LF heel/sole, excluding the steel jaw above it.
  peek=rect(134,150,141,150)|rect(130,151,141,151)|rect(128,152,141,152)|rect(125,153,141,153)|rect(122,154,141,158)
  body[peek]=0
  # The whole jaw remains a foreground occluder of LF in every pose.
  jaw=b.piece(ref,b.mask([(101,134),(106,129),(111,130),(113,140),(118,142),(140,137),(146,133),(145,143),(139,149),(129,153),(116,155),(105,149),(100,143)]))
  jaw[peek]=0
  b.comp(body,jaw)
 # Paint the trunk behind each socket before any limb moves. These small patches
 # continue existing purple shade bands and cannot create an inter-leg bridge.
 patches={'e':[(55,140,68,145),(96,141,111,146)],'se':[(49,116,57,120),(76,137,90,143)],'ne':[(92,141,104,148),(122,119,132,125),(51,133,59,140)]}[v]
 for x0,y0,x1,y1 in patches:
  for y in range(y0,y1+1):
   for x in range(x0,x1+1):
    if not body[y,x,3]:body[y,x]=P['26183f']
 # Keep every complete native disc/armour pixel on the pinned layer.
 b.comp(body,b.piece(ref,discs))
 for n,pp in parts.items():
  q=D[v][n];q['sole']=b.support(pp['lower']).tolist()
  for p,a in pp.items():save(a,f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
 save(body,f'source/{v}_body.png');save(b.piece(ref,discs),f'source/{v}_discs.png')
 return ref,parts,body,discs

def band(a,p0,p1,width,far=False):
 # Hand-defined steel profile: lit left bevel, middle steel, dark right seam.
 # The centre follows integer stair steps; no rotation, interpolation or scaling.
 x0,y0=map(int,p0);x1,y1=map(int,p1)
 if y1<y0:x0,y0,x1,y1=x1,y1,x0,y0
 for y in range(y0,y1+1):
  t=(y-y0)/max(1,y1-y0);cx=int(np.floor(x0+(x1-x0)*t+.5));l=cx-width//2;r=l+width
  a[y,l:r+1]=P['211e27'];a[y,l+1:r]=P['4e4b55' if far else '8a8892'];a[y,l+1:l+3]=P['8a8892' if far else 'c0bfc6'];a[y,r-1:r]=P['3d3a44']

def casing(a,p0,p1,width,far=False):
 x0,y0=map(int,p0);x1,y1=map(int,p1)
 if y1<y0:x0,y0,x1,y1=x1,y1,x0,y0
 for y in range(y0,y1+1):
  cx=int(np.floor(x0+(x1-x0)*(y-y0)/max(1,y1-y0)+.5));l=cx-width//2;r=l+width
  a[y,l:r+1]=P['211e27'];a[y,l+1:r]=P['26183f'];a[y,l+1:l+3]=P['26183f' if far else '4a347e']

def articulation(q,delta,dip,body,far):
 # The socket can pivot under its fixed round housing. Choose integer knee and
 # hidden attachment points that retain the two native link lengths, rather
 # than shortening a lifted leg into a straight telescoping rod.
 r0=np.array(q['root']);k0=np.array(q['k']);j0=np.array(q['join']);j=j0+delta
 l1=np.linalg.norm(k0-r0);l2=np.linalg.norm(j0-k0);half=np.floor((delta+[0,dip])*.5+.5).astype(int)
 best=None
 for rx in range(-3,4):
  for ry in range(-3,4):
   root=r0+[rx,ry+dip]
   if not far and not body[tuple((root-[0,dip])[::-1])][3]:continue
   for kx in range(-3,4):
    for ky in range(-2,3):
     kd=half+[kx,ky];kn=k0+kd;u=np.linalg.norm(kn-root);lo=np.linalg.norm(j-kn);err=abs(u+lo-l1-l2)
     if abs(u-l1)>1 or abs(lo-l2)>1 or err>1:continue
     score=(kx*kx+ky*ky)*4+rx*rx+ry*ry+err*.1
     if best is None or score<best[0]:best=(score,root,kn,kd,float(err),float(u-l1),float(lo-l2))
 if best is None:
  root=r0+[0,dip];kn=k0+half;return root,kn,half,float(abs(np.linalg.norm(kn-root)+np.linalg.norm(j-kn)-l1-l2)),None,None
 return best[1:]

def joint_cleanup(a,ref,body,parts,rig,dip):
 # Finish only the small shading/seam fragments at redrawn moving joins.
 # Approved body/discs, alpha, knee cores and the complete foot pads are locked.
 from collections import Counter
 from scipy.ndimage import label
 structure=np.ones((3,3),np.uint8)
 def colour_count(im,c):
  lab,num=label(np.all(im[120:165]==c,axis=2),structure);sizes=np.bincount(lab.ravel())[1:];return int(((sizes>0)&(sizes<=2)).sum())
 def bits(im):
  out=[]
  for c in set(map(tuple,im[im[:,:,3]>0])):
   lab,num=label(np.all(im[120:165]==c,axis=2),structure);sizes=np.bincount(lab.ravel())
   for i in np.where((sizes>0)&(sizes<=2))[0]:
    if i:
     yy,xx=np.where(lab==i);out.append((c,list(zip((yy+120).tolist(),xx.tolist()))))
  return out
 mutable=np.zeros((192,192),bool);kcore=np.zeros_like(mutable)
 for rr in rig:
  n=rr['foot'];dx,dy=rr['delta'];src=parts[n]['lower'];ys,xs=np.where(src[:,:,3]>0)
  mutable|=(X>=xs.min()+dx-2)&(X<=xs.max()+dx+2)&(Y>=ys.min()+dy-4)&(Y<rr['source_sole'][1]+dy-8)
  kn=b.shift(parts[n]['knee'],*rr['knee_delta']);kcore|=np.logical_or.reduce([np.all(kn==P[h],axis=2) for h in ['e4e4ea','c0bfc6','8a8892']])
 pinned=b.shift(body,0,dip);mutable&=~((pinned[:,:,3]>0)&np.all(a==pinned,axis=2));mutable&=(a[:,:,3]>0)&~kcore
 limit=len(bits(ref))+2;edits=[]
 ramps=[set(P[h] for h in ['141218','211e27','26183f','3d3a44','4e4b55']),set(P[h] for h in ['4e4b55','8a8892','c0bfc6','e4e4ea']),set(P[h] for h in ['26183f','4a347e','7258b4','a990e0'])]
 for _ in range(80):
  pcs=bits(a);count=len(pcs)
  if count<=limit:break
  best=None
  for c,cc in pcs:
   if not all(mutable[y,x] for y,x in cc):continue
   ramp=set().union(*(r for r in ramps if c in r));near=Counter(tuple(a[yy,xx]) for y,x in cc for yy in range(y-1,y+2) for xx in range(x-1,x+2) if (yy,xx) not in cc and a[yy,xx,3])
   for colour,num in near.most_common(3):
    if colour==c or colour not in ramp:continue
    trial=a.copy()
    for y,x in cc:trial[y,x]=colour
    score=colour_count(a,c)+colour_count(a,colour)-colour_count(trial,c)-colour_count(trial,colour)
    key=(score,-len(cc),num)
    if score>0 and (best is None or key>best[0]):best=(key,cc,colour)
  if best is None:break
  _,cc,colour=best
  for y,x in cc:a[y,x]=colour;edits.append([x,y,bytes(colour[:3]).hex()])
 return edits

def make(v):
 for d in ['source','walk/frames','walk/layers']:(R/d).mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 ref,parts,body,discs=sources(v);frames=[];rig=[]
 for f in range(8):
  dip=int(f in [0,4]);layers={}
  for n,pp in parts.items():
   q=D[v][n];sole=np.array(q['sole']);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f];vt=T['views'][v]
   target=np.array(vt['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre']) if n in vt['visible_feet'] else sole+off*np.array(vt['u'])*vt['f']-[0,lift]
   # Round the ground position once, then subtract the integer lift.
   delta=np.floor(target+[0,lift]-sole+.5).astype(int)-[0,lift];dx,dy=map(int,delta)
   root,kn,kd,length_error,upper_error,lower_error=articulation(q,delta,dip,body,n.startswith('L'));jn=np.array(q['join'])+delta
   a=np.zeros_like(ref)
   casing(a,root,kn,q['w']+4,n.startswith('L'));casing(a,kn,jn,q['w']+3,n.startswith('L'))
   band(a,root,kn,q['w'],n.startswith('L'));band(a,kn,jn,q['w']-1,n.startswith('L'))
   raw=b.shift(pp['lower'],dx,dy);b.comp(a,raw);b.comp(a,b.shift(pp['knee'],*kd))
   bottom=int(sole[1]+dy);a[Y>bottom]=0;a[(Y>=bottom-1)&(raw[:,:,3]==0)]=0
   # Save the complete joint even where its fixed housing occludes it.
   layers[n]=a;save(a,f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png')
   rig.append(dict(foot=n,frame=f,delta=[dx,dy],knee_delta=kd.tolist(),source_sole=sole.tolist(),target=target.tolist(),lift=lift,hidden_in_still=n not in vt['visible_feet'],root=root.tolist(),knee=kn.tolist(),ankle=jn.tolist(),leg_length_error=length_error,upper_link_error=upper_error,lower_link_error=lower_error))
  a=np.zeros_like(ref)
  for n in (['LF','LH'] if v=='ne' else ['LH','LF']):b.comp(a,layers[n])
  b.comp(a,b.shift(body,0,dip))
  for n in ['RH','RF']:b.comp(a,layers[n])
  b.comp(a,b.shift(b.piece(ref,discs),0,dip))
  # Continue the flank behind a receding far-hind socket. These are only the
  # missing interior socket pixels, never the open space between the four feet.
  if v in ['se','ne']:
   sf=R.parents[2]/'walk/tools/walk_tools.py';tr=ast.parse(sf.read_text());ns={'np':np}
   exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in tr.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='find_slits'],type_ignores=[]),str(sf),'exec'),ns)
   for x,y in ns['find_slits'](a,ref,dip):
    if (v=='ne' and 50<=x<=63 and 138+dip<=y<=145+dip) or (v=='se' and 53<=x<=57 and 119+dip<=y<=124+dip):a[y,x]=P['26183f']
  # Only enclosed metal sockets are filled. Real spaces between legs stay open.
  holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0);st=b.shift(ref,0,dip)
  for y,x in zip(*np.where(holes)):a[y,x]=st[y,x] if st[y,x,3] else P['26183f']
  cut={'e':140,'se':116,'ne':119}[v]+dip;a[:cut]=st[:cut]
  edits=joint_cleanup(a,ref,body,parts,[q for q in rig if q['frame']==f],dip)
  # Any tiny disconnected trace is a contour remnant, never a new floating part.
  for cc in b.pieces(a[:,:,3]>0)[1:]:
   if len(cc)<=2:
    for y,x in cc:a[y,x]=0
  (R/f'walk/layers/{v}_join_edits_f{f}.json').write_text(json.dumps(edits))
  frames.append(a);save(a,f'walk/frames/{v}_f{f}.png')
 rigfile=R/'rig.json';allrig=json.loads(rigfile.read_text()) if rigfile.exists() else {};allrig[v]=rig;rigfile.write_text(json.dumps(allrig,indent=2))
 report=R/'REPORT.txt';lines=report.read_text().splitlines() if report.exists() else ['hippo-walk35-run1; tokens: unavailable.','RF/RH = near right; LF/LH = far left. Labels checked against stills; retained.']
 lines=[s for s in lines if not s.startswith(v+':')];lines.append(v+': 8 raw frames drawn; native steel joints/feet, fixed discs/body; audit pending.');report.write_text('\n'.join(lines)+'\n')
 print(v,'source soles',{n:q['sole'] for n,q in D[v].items()});boards(v,ref,frames)
 return frames

 selout=b.load_selout();font=ImageFont.truetype('C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf',17);bg=(65,73,80,255)

FILE walk\hippo_l\run1\native.py
from pathlib import Path
import ast
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageFont
from collections import deque
R=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
B=R.parents[2]
def fill_holes(op):
    seen = op.copy()
    queue = deque()
    for x in range(192):
        for y in (0, 191):
            if not seen[y, x]:
                seen[y, x] = True
                queue.append((y, x))
    for y in range(192):
        for x in (0, 191):
            if not seen[y, x]:
                seen[y, x] = True
                queue.append((y, x))
    while queue:
        y, x = queue.popleft()
        for yy, xx in ((y - 1, x), (y + 1, x), (y, x - 1), (y, x + 1)):
            if 0 <= yy < 192 and 0 <= xx < 192 and (not seen[yy, xx]):
                seen[yy, xx] = True
                queue.append((yy, xx))
    return ~seen | op

def mask(poly):
    im = Image.new('1', (192, 192))
    ImageDraw.Draw(im).polygon(poly, fill=1)
    return np.array(im)

def shift(a, dx=0, dy=0):
    b = np.zeros_like(a)
    ys, xs = np.where(a[:, :, 3] > 0)
    xx = xs + int(dx)
    yy = ys + int(dy)
    ok = (xx >= 0) & (xx < 192) & (yy >= 0) & (yy < 192)
    b[yy[ok], xx[ok]] = a[ys[ok], xs[ok]]
    return b

def comp(a, b):
    m = b[:, :, 3] > 0
    a[m] = b[m]

def piece(a, m):
    b = a.copy()
    b[~m] = 0
    return b

def support(a):
    yy, xx = np.where(a[:, :, 3] > 0)
    row = int(yy.max())
    xs = np.where(a[row - 1:row + 1, :, 3].any(axis=0))[0]
    return np.array([(xs.min() + xs.max()) / 2, row], float)

def load_selout():
    sp = B / 'rhino_l/take-a/anim/selout_rhino.py'
    tree = ast.parse(sp.read_text())
    ns = {'np': np, 'OUTLINE': np.array([20, 26, 22], np.uint8)}
    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == 'selout'], type_ignores=[]), str(sp), 'exec'), ns)
    return ns['selout']

def pieces(op):
    seen = np.zeros_like(op)
    out = []
    for y, x in zip(*np.where(op)):
        if seen[y, x]:
            continue
        todo = [(int(y), int(x))]
        seen[y, x] = True
        pts = []
        while todo:
            yy, xx = todo.pop()
            pts.append((yy, xx))
            for dy in (-1, 0, 1):
                for dx in (-1, 0, 1):
                    ny, nx = (yy + dy, xx + dx)
                    if 0 <= ny < 192 and 0 <= nx < 192 and op[ny, nx] and (not seen[ny, nx]):
                        seen[ny, nx] = True
                        todo.append((ny, nx))
        out.append(pts)
    return sorted(out, key=len, reverse=True)

def locate(a, src):
    sole = b.support(src)
    ys, xs = np.where((src[:, :, 3] > 0) & (b.Y >= sole[1] - 5))
    cols = src[ys, xs]
    best = None
    for dy in range(-12, 13):
        for dx in range(-14, 15):
            score = float(np.all(a[ys + dy, xs + dx] == cols, axis=1).mean())
            key = (score, -abs(dx) - abs(dy))
            if best is None or key > best[0]:
                best = (key, dx, dy)
    (_, score_unused), dx, dy = best
    score = best[0][0]
    sy = int(sole[1])
    by, bx = np.where((src[:, :, 3] > 0) & (b.Y >= sy - 1))
    left, right = (int(bx.min()), int(bx.max()))
    ends = []
    for x in [left, right]:
        ends.append(any((np.array_equal(a[y + dy, x + dx], src[y, x]) for y in [sy - 1, sy] if src[y, x, 3] > 0)))
    return (sole + [dx, dy], score, [dx, dy], all(ends))
Y,X=np.indices((192,192))

b=sys.modules[__name__]

def specks(a,details=False):
 out=[]
 for c in set(map(tuple,a[a[:,:,3]>0])):
  mask=np.all(a==c,axis=2)&(Y>=120)&(Y<=164)
  for cc in b.pieces(mask):
   if len(cc)<=2:out.append((bytes(c[:3]).hex(),cc))
 return out if details else len(out)

def audit():
 lines=['hippo-walk35-run1 independent pixel audit.',
 'Reused ape_l/run1 native.py locate(): exhaustive sole colour-stamp translations, no rig/target used in search.',
 'Sole = outer endpoints of bottom TWO painted foot rows; hidden/covered stamps measured on saved leg layer.',
 'Specks = 8-connected single-colour components of 1 or 2 pixels, restricted to rows120-164 inclusive.',

 res={};fails=[];font=ImageFont.truetype('C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf',15)

   sl=find_slits(a,ref,dip)

   sr=dict(frame=f,canvas=list(a.shape[:2]),binary_alpha=bool(np.isin(a[:,:,3],[0,255]).all()),off_palette_pixels=sum(tuple(c) not in allowed for c in a[op]),colours=len(set(map(tuple,a[op]))),components=len(cc),stray_pixels=sum(map(len,cc[1:])),holes=int((b.fill_holes(op)&~op).sum()),edge=bool(op[[0,-1]].any() or op[:,[0,-1]].any()),lowest_row=int(np.where(op)[0].max()),upper_canvas_diff_percent=float(100*d.sum()/d.size),upper_canvas_diff_pixels=int(d.sum()),disc_diff_pixels=int(np.any(a[dp]!=dm[dp],axis=1).sum()),specks=specks(a),seams=sl,body_slits=body_slits)
   vr['structure'].append(sr);lines.append('STRUCT '+v+' '+json.dumps(sr))

    if not ok:fails.append(f'{v} f{f} {key}')

   for x,y in sl:marked[y,x]=(255,50,50,255)
   tile=Image.fromarray(marked).crop((35,95,151,165)).resize((580,350),Image.Resampling.NEAREST);base=Image.new('RGBA',tile.size,(65,73,80));base.alpha_composite(tile);seamboard.paste(base.convert('RGB'),((f+1)*580,25));ds.text(((f+1)*580+8,3),v+f' f{f}: {len(sl)} flags',font=font,fill='white')
  tile=Image.fromarray(ref).crop((35,95,151,165)).resize((580,350),Image.Resampling.NEAREST);base=Image.new('RGBA',tile.size,(65,73,80));base.alpha_composite(tile);seamboard.paste(base.convert('RGB'),(0,25));ds.text((8,3),v+' still',font=font,fill='white');seamboard.save(R/f'{v}_seams_review.png')
  footpic=Image.new('RGB',(1480,4*270),(58,66,62));dr=ImageDraw.Draw(footpic)
  for j,n in enumerate(['RF','LH','LF','RH']):
   src=load(R/f'source/{v}_{n}_lower.png');sole=b.support(src);tracks=[];pp=[]
   for f,a in enumerate(frames):
    layer=load(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png');p,score,d,ends=b.locate(a,src);where='composite'
    if score<1 or not ends:p,score,d,ends=b.locate(layer,src);where='leg layer (occluded)'
    rr=next(q for q in rig if q['foot']==n and q['frame']==f);target=np.array(rr['target']);err=np.abs(p-target);lift=rr['lift'];actual_lift=int(np.floor(target[1]+lift+.5))-p[1]
    sy,sx=np.where(src[:,:,3]>0);dx,dy=d;core=sy>=sy.min()+4;match=float(np.all(layer[sy[core]+dy,sx[core]+dx]==src[sy[core],sx[core]],axis=1).mean())
    pad=sy>=sole[1]-7;padmatch=float(np.all(layer[sy[pad]+dy,sx[pad]+dx]==src[sy[pad],sx[pad]],axis=1).mean())
    knee=load(R/f'source/{v}_{n}_knee.png');yy,xx=np.where(knee[:,:,3]>0);kd=rr['knee_delta'];kneematch=float(np.all(layer[yy+kd[1],xx+kd[0]]==knee[yy,xx],axis=1).mean())
    tr=dict(frame=f,sole=p.tolist(),measured_from=where,stamp_match=score,endpoint_visible=ends,delta=d,target=target.tolist(),target_error_xy=err.tolist(),expected_lift=lift,measured_lift=float(actual_lift),native_lower_core_match=match,native_foot_pad_match=padmatch,native_knee_match=kneematch,articulated_length_error=rr['leg_length_error'],upper_link_error=rr['upper_link_error'],lower_link_error=rr['lower_link_error'])
    pp.append(p);tracks.append(tr);lines.append('CONTACT '+v+' '+n+' '+json.dumps(tr))
    if err.max()>1 or actual_lift!=lift or score!=1 or not ends:fails.append(f'{v} {n} f{f} contact')
    if padmatch!=1 or kneematch!=1 or rr['leg_length_error']>1 or rr['upper_link_error'] is None:fails.append(f'{v} {n} f{f} native parts/length')
   start=(T['gait'][n]+3)%8;order=[(start+k)%8 for k in range(5)];step=np.array(T['views'][v]['planted_step_px']);locked=np.array([pp[f]-k*step for k,f in enumerate(order)]);spread=np.ptp(locked,axis=0)
   vr['feet'][n]=dict(planted_order=order,spread_xy=spread.tolist(),max_target_error=float(max(max(t['target_error_xy']) for t in tracks)),frames=tracks)
   if spread.max()>2:fails.append(f'{v} {n} lock')
   oy=j*270;dr.text((10,oy+4),f'{v} {n}: planted {order}; pixel-measured spread {spread[0]:.3f}/{spread[1]:.3f}px',font=font,fill='white');colors=['#ffb575','#75e5cf','#e99fe2','#c9e978','#aab8ff'];orig=locked.mean(0)
   for k,f in enumerate(order):
    dr.text((10,oy+26+k*20),f'f{f} sole {pp[f][0]:.1f},{pp[f][1]:.1f} locked {locked[k][0]:.3f},{locked[k][1]:.3f} {tracks[f]["measured_from"]}',fill=colors[k],font=font)
    dx,dy=tracks[f]['delta'];yy,xx=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(Y>=sole[1]-1))
    for y,x in zip(yy,xx):
     pt=np.array([x+dx,y+dy])-k*step;px,py=np.floor((pt-orig)*7+[1200,oy+65]).astype(int);dr.rectangle((px,py,px+6,py+6),fill=colors[k])
   for f in range(8):
    dr.text((10+f*180,oy+139),f'f{f} '+('layer' if 'layer' in tracks[f]['measured_from'] else 'frame'),font=font,fill='white');cx,cy=sole;box=(int(cx)-17,int(cy)-19,int(cx)+19,int(cy)+9);where=load(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png') if 'layer' in tracks[f]['measured_from'] else frames[f];tile=Image.new('RGBA',(144,112),(73,79,75));tile.alpha_composite(Image.fromarray(where).crop(box).resize((144,112),Image.Resampling.NEAREST));footpic.paste(tile.convert('RGB'),(10+f*180,oy+158))
  footpic.save(R/f'{v}_footprints.png');lines.append('SUMMARY '+v+' '+json.dumps({n:{k:z for k,z in q.items() if k!='frames'} for n,q in vr['feet'].items()}))
 res['failures']=fails;(R/'verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(res,indent=2));lines.append('FAILURES '+repr(fails));(R/'checks.txt').write_text('\n'.join(lines)+'\n')
 for v in ['e','se','ne']:
  vr=res[v];print(v,'specks',vr['still_specks'],[s['specks'] for s in vr['structure']],'slits',[len(s['seams']) for s in vr['structure']],'body',[len(s['body_slits']) for s in vr['structure']],'discs',[s['disc_diff_pixels'] for s in vr['structure']]);print('feet',{n:(q['spread_xy'],q['max_target_error']) for n,q in vr['feet'].items()})
 print('FAILURES',fails)
 return res

FILE walk\hippo_l\run2\brief.txt
You are making pixel-art walk frames for a game. Write only inside D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\hippo_l\run2.
Read anything under D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba. Do not touch any other folder (run1 included), assets, art or
tools, and do not commit. Run tag: hippo-walk35-run2.

This is run 2 of the violet L hippo's four-beat walk at 35 degrees. Run 1 (D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\hippo_l\run1)
made the e, se and ne views. Read run1\brief.txt (the machine, the gait, the method, the rules and the pass marks all
still apply), run1\REPORT.txt, and run1\build.py, native.py, rig.json, verify.py and checks.txt: that is your own method
from run 1, so reuse it rather than starting again. Read D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\WALK_PLAYBOOK.md section 7
for the front and back views, and the fennec's run 2 (D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run2: brief.txt,
REPORT.txt, frontback.py), which drew s and n the same way. The stills on the 192 canvas are in run2\stills; the targets
(the same plan as run 1, with s and n included) are in run2\targets.json, and the guides are run2\guide_s.png and
guide_n.png. Draw s and n only. Do not redraw e, se or ne; the walk lane cleans those itself.

Keep run2\REPORT.txt up to date after each view, so a report exists even if the run is stopped by its token cap.

What the walk lane found in run 1, so do not repeat it: in se and ne the body layer (run1\source\<view>_body.png) was
filled where a leg had been in the still, including below the belly, so dark body colour hangs below the belly with
straight edges where a leg moved away, and in ne that fill was drawn over the far hind foot as a dark vertical line
splitting the foot. The body stops at the belly: where a leg in the still came out below the belly, the body layer there
is transparent, not filled. Fill only inside the body's own outline, continuing its bands and seams.

Part 1, s. Part 2, n. Each is an 8-frame loop, 125 ms a frame, with run 1's gait: LF swings f1-f3, LH f3-f5, RF f5-f7,
RH f7, f0, f1 (wrapping), lifts 2, 3 and 2 px; the body dips 1 px on f0 and f4. Per view, the path turns into screen px as
offset * f * u, with the lift straight up:
  s: u = (0, 1), f = 0.5736; a planted foot moves (0.00, -1.72) px a frame on the canvas (x, y; y down)
  n: u = (0, -1), f = 0.5736; a planted foot moves (0.00, +1.72) px a frame on the canvas
At 35 degrees a step toward or away from the camera shows at 0.57 of its length, so these views need real foot travel up
and down the screen, not reordered side frames. Round each frame's position so a planted foot's total travel stays within
0.5 px of the target. The labels are in D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\hippo_l\feet.json. In s the front feet are
the near feet on row 160 (RF on screen left, LF on screen right), and the hind feet are hidden behind the head and front
legs; in n the hind feet are near on row 160 (LH on screen left, RH on screen right), and the front feet are hidden behind
the rump. Draw a hidden foot only where the new pose shows it. A foot nearer the camera than in the still may sit up to
4 px below row 160.
Draw order: in s, hind legs, then body, then front legs, then the head and jaw exactly as the still layers them over the
front legs (look at the still at 8x where the jaw meets each front leg, and keep that); in n, front legs, then body, then
hind legs, then any tail or rump part the still shows over the hind legs. In both views each leg moves straight up and
down the screen with its knee joint, joints redrawn by hand; the big hip and shoulder discs stay pinned to the body and
dip with it; the legs stay apart from each other and from the jaw. Every part is the still's own pixels translated, never
rotated or resampled.

Pass marks, for s and n: as in run1\brief.txt (foot lock within 2 px measured from the frame pixels; each visible foot on
its target within 1 px; lifts exact; above the legs every frame equals the still after the dip; the stills' 14 colours
only; binary alpha; no holes, stray pixels or edge contact; specks per frame no more than the still's count plus 2), and
these two new ones:
- Belly: per view, for each leg's column span in the still, the body layer's lowest opaque row in that span may not sit
  more than 1 px below the straight line between the body's lowest rows in the columns just outside the span. Report it.
- Whole feet: every foot pad in every frame is the still's pad translated whole, with no line or other part drawn across
  it except where the still itself shows something in front of it (report the pad match per foot per frame).
Run these and report their results:
  python D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\tools\walk_tools.py footlock
    --targets D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\hippo_l\run2\targets.json

    --still-dir D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\hippo_l\run2\stills

    --still-dir D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\hippo_l\run2\stills --body a990e0 7258b4 4a347e 26183f
Seams flags between two legs or between a leg and the jaw are real gaps and stay open. If the silhouette footlock merges a
sole with the jaw or body, say so and give your own stamp measure as the deciding one.

Output in run2: walk\frames\s_f0.png to s_f7.png and n_f0.png to n_f7.png (raw, no outline, like the stills), walk\layers
and source\ as in run 1, a preview gif per view (125 ms, loop), footprint pictures, board.png (per view: the still, then

still, discs and jaw whole, nothing hanging below the belly?

FILE walk\hippo_l\run2\build.py
"""hippo-walk35-run2: run1 native parts, fennec run2 depth ordering.
All writes are relative to this run2 directory. Run with python -B.
"""
import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode=True
from pathlib import Path
import json, importlib.util
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageFont
R=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(R.parent/'run1'))
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('run1_build',R.parent/'run1/build.py')
old=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(old)
b=old.b;P=old.P;X=old.X;Y=old.Y;load=old.load
T=json.loads((R/'targets.json').read_text())
save=lambda a,p:Image.fromarray(a).save(R/p)
def rect(x0,y0,x1,y1):return (X>=x0)&(X<=x1)&(Y>=y0)&(Y<=y1)

def sources(v):
    ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');parts={};used=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
    if v=='s':
        # Native jaw contour, traced at 8x. This foreground piece is NEVER
        # assigned to either foot or knee, including its charcoal underside.
        jaw=np.zeros_like(used)
        left={134:71,135:71,136:71,137:70,138:70,139:70,140:70,141:70,142:70,143:70,144:70,145:70,146:71,147:71,148:71,149:71,150:71,151:72,152:73,153:74,154:75,155:76,156:77}
        for y,l in left.items():jaw[y,l:192-l]=True
        for n,x0,x1 in [('RF',50,85),('LF',106,141)]:
            knee=rect(x0,138,x1,149)&~jaw
            lower=rect(x0,150,x1,160)&~jaw
            # No background and no jaw paint enters the moving sources.
            parts[n]={'knee':b.piece(ref,knee),'lower':b.piece(ref,lower)}
            used|=knee|lower
        # The main shoulder housings (above row138) remain pinned. Front
        # upper links emerge beneath them, beside the tusks and jaw.
        defs={'RF':dict(root=(65,134),k=(65,145),join=(66,151),w=5),
              'LF':dict(root=(126,134),k=(126,145),join=(125,151),w=5)}
        cutoff=111
    else:
        # Rear discs end at row140. The steel joint, dark casing and whole
        # toe/heel assembly beneath them move; no leg-shaped body patches.
        for n,x0,x1 in [('LH',58,86),('RH',105,133)]:
            knee=rect(x0,141,x1,150)
            lower=rect(x0,151,x1,160)
            parts[n]={'knee':b.piece(ref,knee),'lower':b.piece(ref,lower)}
            used|=knee|lower
        defs={'LH':dict(root=(71,139),k=(71,146),join=(72,152),w=6),
              'RH':dict(root=(120,139),k=(120,146),join=(119,152),w=6)}
        cutoff=128
    pinned=b.piece(ref,~used)
    # Separate the trunk from its fixed foreground assemblies. In s the
    # head and jaw conceal the trunk; in n the rump/tail lies over the legs.
    body=b.piece(pinned,Y<=cutoff)
    foreground=b.piece(pinned,Y>cutoff)
    # Hidden native feet are translated dark-ramp counterparts. Their entire
    # layers are kept for measurement; depth compositing reveals only pixels
    # which actually clear the original head/body/near legs.
    pairs=[('RH','RF'),('LH','LF')] if v=='s' else [('LF','LH'),('RF','RH')]
    for n,src in pairs:
        dy=-25 if v=='s' else -28
        parts[n]={p:old.dark(b.shift(a,0,dy)) for p,a in parts[src].items()}
        defs[n]={k:(tuple(np.array(z)+[0,dy]) if k in ['root','k','join'] else z) for k,z in defs[src].items()}
    for n,pp in parts.items():
        defs[n]['sole']=b.support(pp['lower']).tolist()
        for p,a in pp.items():save(a,f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
    save(body,f'source/{v}_body.png');save(foreground,f'source/{v}_foreground.png')
    # Discs/head/rump stay complete in the foreground; this also documents
    # the exact fixed pixels separately from the trunk belly audit.
    save(pinned,f'source/{v}_pinned.png')
    return ref,parts,body,foreground,defs

def make(v):
    for d in ['source','walk/frames','walk/layers']:(R/d).mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    ref,parts,body,foreground,D=sources(v);frames=[];rig=[]
    for f in range(8):
        dip=int(f in [0,4]);ll={}
        for n,pp in parts.items():
            q=D[n];sole=np.array(q['sole']);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f];vt=T['views'][v]
            target=np.array(vt['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre']) if n in vt['visible_feet'] else sole+off*np.array(vt['u'])*vt['f']-[0,lift]
            delta=np.floor(target+[0,lift]-sole+.5).astype(int)-[0,lift]
            # Depth only: the native pad centre can differ half a pixel from
            # a label; never shift a leg sideways to compensate for that.
            delta[0]=0
            dx,dy=map(int,delta);kd=np.array([0,int(np.floor((dy+dip)*.5+.5))])
            socket=np.array(q['root'])+[0,dip]
            # The attachment slides inside the pinned housing in this depth
            # projection. Keep the two-link projected length native while the
            # exposed knee moves half the foot travel. The socket-to-root
            # connector is concealed by the housing, apart from its seam.
            root=np.array(q['root'])+delta;kn=np.array(q['k'])+kd;jn=np.array(q['join'])+delta
            native_length=np.linalg.norm(np.array(q['k'])-q['root'])+np.linalg.norm(np.array(q['join'])-q['k'])
            length_error=float(abs(np.linalg.norm(kn-root)+np.linalg.norm(jn-kn)-native_length))
            far=n.endswith('H') if v=='s' else n.endswith('F')
            a=np.zeros_like(ref)
            old.casing(a,socket,root,q['w']+3,far);old.casing(a,root,kn,q['w']+3,far);old.casing(a,kn,jn,q['w']+3,far)
            old.band(a,socket,root,q['w'],far);old.band(a,root,kn,q['w'],far);old.band(a,kn,jn,q['w'],far)
            # Continue the native casing's full bevel/seam profile across a
            # one- or two-row downward extension. A narrow centre band alone
            # would leave a horizontal cut in the wide inner casing of n.
            ky,kx=np.where(pp['knee'][:,:,3]>0);ly,lx=np.where(pp['lower'][:,:,3]>0)
            boundary=int(ky.max());top=int(ly.min())
            cols=np.where(pp['knee'][boundary,:,3]>0)[0]
            for row in range(boundary+int(kd[1])+1,top+dy):
                a[row,cols]=pp['knee'][boundary,cols]
            b.comp(a,b.shift(pp['knee'],*kd));raw=b.shift(pp['lower'],dx,dy);b.comp(a,raw)
            bottom=int(sole[1]+dy);a[Y>bottom]=0;a[(Y>=bottom-1)&(raw[:,:,3]==0)]=0
            ll[n]=a;save(a,f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png')
            rig.append(dict(foot=n,frame=f,delta=delta.tolist(),knee_delta=kd.tolist(),source_sole=sole.tolist(),target=target.tolist(),lift=lift,root=root.tolist(),socket=socket.tolist(),knee=kn.tolist(),ankle=jn.tolist(),native_projected_length=float(native_length),leg_length_error=length_error,hidden_in_still=n not in vt['visible_feet']))
        a=np.zeros_like(ref);far=['RH','LH'] if v=='s' else ['LF','RF'];near=['RF','LF'] if v=='s' else ['LH','RH']
        for n in far:b.comp(a,ll[n])
        b.comp(a,b.shift(body,0,dip))
        # Housing foreground is drawn before near legs, then its actual
        # fixed pixels are restored; gaps in the native outline remain open.
        b.comp(a,b.shift(foreground,0,dip))
        for n in near:b.comp(a,ll[n])
        b.comp(a,b.shift(foreground,0,dip))
        st=b.shift(ref,0,dip);cut=(138 if v=='s' else 141)+dip
        a[:cut]=st[:cut]
        # Only enclosed join holes; no automatic slit fill in open leg gaps.
        holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0)
        for y,x in zip(*np.where(holes)):a[y,x]=st[y,x] if st[y,x,3] else P['211e27']
        protected=b.shift(body,0,dip);b.comp(protected,b.shift(foreground,0,dip))
        for n in near:
            rr=next(q for q in rig if q['frame']==f and q['foot']==n)
            b.comp(protected,b.shift(parts[n]['lower'],*rr['delta']))
        # Cleanup is limited to redrawn connectors; every fixed pixel and
        # every exposed native pad pixel is locked against recolouring.
        b.comp(protected,b.shift(foreground,0,dip))
        edits=old.joint_cleanup(a,ref,b.shift(protected,0,-dip),parts,[q for q in rig if q['frame']==f],dip)
        save(a,f'walk/frames/{v}_f{f}.png');save(b.shift(body,0,dip),f'walk/layers/{v}_body_f{f}.png');save(b.shift(foreground,0,dip),f'walk/layers/{v}_foreground_f{f}.png')
        (R/f'walk/layers/{v}_join_edits_f{f}.json').write_text(json.dumps(edits))
        frames.append(a)
    path=R/'rig.json';allrig=json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else {};allrig[v]=rig;path.write_text(json.dumps(allrig,indent=2))
    report=R/'REPORT.txt';lines=report.read_text().splitlines() if report.exists() else ['hippo-walk35-run2; tokens: unavailable.','Reused run1 native translations/joints and fennec run2 front/back depth order.']
    lines=[s for s in lines if not s.startswith(v+':')];lines.append(v+': 8 native frames and source/layers saved; audit pending.');report.write_text('\n'.join(lines)+'\n')
    old.R=R;old.boards(v,ref,frames)
    print(v,'source soles',{n:q['sole'] for n,q in D.items()})
    return frames

def combine():
    for suffix in ['board.png','legs_zoom.png']:
        ims=[Image.open(R/f'{v}_{suffix}').convert('RGB') for v in ['s','n']]
        out=Image.new('RGB',(max(i.width for i in ims),sum(i.height for i in ims)));y=0
        for im in ims:out.paste(im,(0,y));y+=im.height
        out.save(R/suffix)
if __name__=='__main__':
    for v in sys.argv[1:] or ['s','n']:make(v)
    if all((R/f'{v}_board.png').exists() for v in ['s','n']):combine()

FILE walk\hippo_l\run2\verify.py
"""Independent exhaustive run1 colour stamps plus trunk belly/full-pad audits."""
import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode=True
import json,ast
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageFont
from scipy.ndimage import label
import build as m
R=m.R;b=m.b;T=m.T;X=m.X;Y=m.Y;load=m.load
tree=ast.parse((R.parents[2]/'walk/tools/walk_tools.py').read_text());ns={'np':np}
exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='find_slits'],type_ignores=[]),'walk_tools.py','exec'),ns)
find_slits=ns['find_slits']
def specks(a):
    total=0
    for c in set(map(tuple,a[a[:,:,3]>0])):
        lab,num=label(np.all(a[120:165]==c,axis=2),np.ones((3,3)))
        sizes=np.bincount(lab.ravel())[1:];total+=int(((sizes>0)&(sizes<=2)).sum())
    return total
def audit():
    lines=['hippo-walk35-run2: independent run1 exhaustive native sole-stamp search.',
    'Full lower/pad = ALL opaque source lower pixels, including native heel and dark recess.',
    'Composite pad exceptions only actual translated fixed foreground overlap; full layer must match 100%.',
    'Body belly = trunk layer, with head/jaw, shoulder/hip discs and rump/tail separately pinned foreground.',
    'No source body fill or contour extension is used. Real leg/jaw gaps remain open.',
    'GIF 120/130ms alternating (1s loop); APNG exact125ms.']
    result={};fails=[];font=ImageFont.truetype('C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf',15)
    rig=json.loads((R/'rig.json').read_text())
    for v in ['s','n']:
        if not (R/f'walk/frames/{v}_f7.png').exists():continue
        ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');frames=[load(R/f'walk/frames/{v}_f{f}.png') for f in range(8)]
        body=load(R/f'source/{v}_body.png');pinned=load(R/f'source/{v}_pinned.png');fg=load(R/f'source/{v}_foreground.png')
        allowed=set(map(tuple,ref[ref[:,:,3]>0]));vr=dict(still_specks=specks(ref),structure=[],feet={},belly=[]);result[v]=vr
        near=['RF','LF'] if v=='s' else ['LH','RH']
        for n in near:
            src=load(R/f'source/{v}_{n}_lower.png');yy,xx=np.where(src[:,:,3]>0);l=int(xx.min());r=int(xx.max())
            # Lowest body rows just outside native leg column span.
            rows=lambda x:np.where(body[:,x,3]>0)[0]
            lr=rows(l-1);rr=rows(r+1)
            # A front foot projects beyond the trunk silhouette; its outer
            # neighbour is transparent. Record that fact rather than inventing
            # a belly pixel. The conservative bound is the trunk's lowest row.
            bound=int(np.where(body[:,:,3]>0)[0].max())
            left=int(lr.max()) if len(lr) else bound;right=int(rr.max()) if len(rr) else bound;ex=[]
            for x in range(l,r+1):
                ys=rows(x)
                if len(ys):ex.append(float(ys.max()-(left+(right-left)*(x-l+1)/(r-l+2))))
            q=dict(foot=n,span=[l,r],outside_rows=[int(lr.max()) if len(lr) else None,int(rr.max()) if len(rr) else None],transparent_exterior_uses_conservative_trunk_bound=bound,max_below_line=max(ex),pass_belly=max(ex)<=1)
            vr['belly'].append(q);lines.append('BELLY '+v+' '+json.dumps(q))
            if not q['pass_belly']:fails.append(f'{v} {n} belly')
        for f,a in enumerate(frames):
            op=a[:,:,3]>0;dip=int(f in [0,4]);st=b.shift(ref,0,dip);cut=(138 if v=='s' else 141)+dip
            d=np.any(a[:cut]!=st[:cut],axis=2);pin=b.shift(pinned,0,dip);mask=pin[:,:,3]>0
            cc=b.pieces(op);sl=find_slits(a,ref,dip);bm=b.shift(body,0,dip)
            q=dict(frame=f,binary_alpha=bool(np.isin(a[:,:,3],[0,255]).all()),off_palette_pixels=sum(tuple(c) not in allowed for c in a[op]),colours=len(set(map(tuple,a[op]))),components=len(cc),stray_pixels=sum(map(len,cc[1:])),holes=int((b.fill_holes(op)&~op).sum()),edge=bool(op[[0,-1]].any() or op[:,[0,-1]].any()),lowest_row=int(np.where(op)[0].max()),upper_diff_percent=float(100*d.sum()/d.size),pinned_diff_pixels=int(np.any(a[mask]!=pin[mask],axis=1).sum()),specks=specks(a),seams=sl,body_slits=[p for p in sl if bm[p[1],p[0],3]])
            vr['structure'].append(q);lines.append('STRUCT '+v+' '+json.dumps(q))
            for key,ok in [('structure',q['components']==1 and not q['holes'] and not q['edge'] and not q['off_palette_pixels'] and q['binary_alpha']),('upper',not d.any()),('pinned',not q['pinned_diff_pixels']),('specks',q['specks']<=vr['still_specks']+2),('body_slits',not q['body_slits'])]:
                if not ok:fails.append(f'{v} f{f} {key}')
        footprint=Image.new('RGB',(1480,1080),(58,66,62));dr=ImageDraw.Draw(footprint)
        for j,n in enumerate(['RF','LH','LF','RH']):
            src=load(R/f'source/{v}_{n}_lower.png');sole=b.support(src);yy,xx=np.where(src[:,:,3]>0);pts=[];tracks=[]
            for f,a in enumerate(frames):
                layer=load(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png');p,score,delta,ends=b.locate(a,src);where='composite'
                if score<1 or not ends:p,score,delta,ends=b.locate(layer,src);where='leg layer (occluded)'
                rr=next(q for q in rig[v] if q['foot']==n and q['frame']==f);target=np.array(rr['target']);dx,dy=delta
                # Exhaustive stamp determines position; rig is used only AFTER
                # locating, to compare target and lift.
                match=np.all(layer[yy+dy,xx+dx]==src[yy,xx],axis=1)
                fgs=b.shift(fg,0,int(f in [0,4]));oc=fgs[yy+dy,xx+dx,3]>0
                cmp=np.all(a[yy+dy,xx+dx]==src[yy,xx],axis=1)
                far=n not in near
                valid=(cmp|oc) if not far else np.ones_like(cmp)
                err=np.abs(p-target);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f]
                ground=sole[1]+int(np.floor(off*T['views'][v]['f']*T['views'][v]['u'][1]+.5))
                measured_lift=float(ground-p[1])
                knee=load(R/f'source/{v}_{n}_knee.png');ky,kx=np.where(knee[:,:,3]>0);kd=rr['knee_delta']
                # A rising whole lower block legitimately covers the bottom
                # of its knee. Compare the remaining native knee pixels only.
                translated=b.shift(src,dx,dy);exposed=translated[ky+kd[1],kx+kd[0],3]==0
                km=float(np.all(layer[ky[exposed]+kd[1],kx[exposed]+kd[0]]==knee[ky[exposed],kx[exposed]],axis=1).mean())
                q=dict(frame=f,sole=p.tolist(),measured_from=where,stamp_match=score,endpoints=ends,delta=delta,target=target.tolist(),target_error_xy=err.tolist(),lift=lift,measured_lift=measured_lift,whole_pad_layer_match=float(match.mean()),whole_pad_frame_match_except_foreground=float(valid.mean()) if not far else 'hidden; layer deciding',foreground_pad_pixels=int(oc.sum()),native_knee_match=km,articulated_length_error=rr['leg_length_error'])
                tracks.append(q);pts.append(p);lines.append('PAD_CONTACT '+v+' '+n+' '+json.dumps(q))
                if err.max()>1 or score!=1 or not ends or measured_lift!=lift:fails.append(f'{v} {n} f{f} contact')
                if not match.all() or not valid.all() or km!=1:fails.append(f'{v} {n} f{f} whole parts')
                if rr['leg_length_error']>1:fails.append(f'{v} {n} f{f} length')
            start=(T['gait'][n]+3)%8;order=[(start+k)%8 for k in range(5)];step=np.array(T['views'][v]['planted_step_px']);locked=np.array([pts[f]-k*step for k,f in enumerate(order)]);spread=np.ptp(locked,axis=0)
            vr['feet'][n]=dict(spread_xy=spread.tolist(),max_target_error=float(max(max(q['target_error_xy']) for q in tracks)),frames=tracks)
            if spread.max()>2:fails.append(f'{v} {n} lock')
            oy=j*270;dr.text((10,oy+4),f'{v} {n} planted {order}; measured spread {spread[0]:.3f}/{spread[1]:.3f}px',font=font,fill='white')
            for k,f in enumerate(order):dr.text((10,oy+28+k*19),f'f{f} sole {pts[f].tolist()} locked {locked[k].round(3).tolist()} {tracks[f]["measured_from"]}',font=font,fill='white')
            for f in range(8):
                box=(int(sole[0])-18,int(sole[1])-21,int(sole[0])+18,int(sole[1])+7)
                source=frames[f] if tracks[f]['measured_from']=='composite' else load(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png')
                tile=Image.new('RGBA',(144,112),(73,79,75));tile.alpha_composite(Image.fromarray(source).crop(box).resize((144,112),Image.Resampling.NEAREST));footprint.paste(tile.convert('RGB'),(10+180*f,oy+157));dr.text((10+180*f,oy+137),f'f{f}',font=font,fill='white')
            colors=['#ffb575','#75e5cf','#e99fe2','#c9e978','#aab8ff'];origin=locked.mean(0)
            for k,f in enumerate(order):
                dx,dy=tracks[f]['delta'];ys,xs=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(Y>=sole[1]-1))
                for y,x in zip(ys,xs):
                    px,py=np.floor((np.array([x+dx,y+dy])-k*step-origin)*6+[1200,oy+50]).astype(int);dr.rectangle((px,py,px+5,py+5),fill=colors[k])
        footprint.save(R/f'{v}_footprints.png')
        print(v,'specks',vr['still_specks'],[s['specks'] for s in vr['structure']],'pinned',[s['pinned_diff_pixels'] for s in vr['structure']])
        print(v,'feet',{n:(q['spread_xy'],q['max_target_error']) for n,q in vr['feet'].items()})
    result['failures']=fails;(R/'verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2));lines.append('FAILURES '+repr(fails));(R/'checks.txt').write_text('\n'.join(lines)+'\n');print('FAILURES',fails)
    return result
if __name__=='__main__':audit()

FILE walk\hippo_l\final\README.txt
Hippo walk at 35 degrees, final from the walk lane (2026-11-05). Preston approved the motion: "Approve, to touch-up".
Machine: hippo_l take-b, still views_cam35_touchup_shade1 (APPROVED, "Keep 1 px version"), copied to stills/.
Frames: frames/<view>_f0.png to _f7.png for s, se, e, ne, n. 192x192, raw (no outline; the packer adds sel-out),
feet row 160 (a planted foot nearer the camera sits up to 163), the still's 14 colours, binary alpha.
Sources: e, se, ne from run1/walk/frames (Codex run 1, 224,713 tokens); s, n from run2/walk/frames (Codex run 2,
100,320 tokens). Total 325,033. Codex's leg layers and body layer are in run1/ and run2/ walk/layers and source/.
Gait: four-beat walk like the rhino (lateral4). LF swings f1-f3, LH f3-f5, RF f5-f7, RH f7-f1, with lifts 2, 3, 2 px.
The body dips 1 px on f0 and f4. 8 fps (125 ms a frame), looping. Step 3 px a frame in e (0.48 m/s at size L).
Planted frames and sole targets for every foot: targets.json (from plan_walk). Foot labels: ../feet.json.
Checks: foot lock within 0.9 px (Codex's stamp measure); walk_tools footlock passes, except se RH, where the sole merges
with the belly (a tool limit). Discs identical to the still, specks within the still's count plus 2.

For touch-up (known defects, coordinates on the 192 canvas, approximate; look at 8x):
- se: dark body fill hangs below the belly beside the near hind leg (RH), a block with a straight edge around x 58-66,
  rows 127-140 (seen on f0, f1 and f5; check all 8). Loose dark specks left of the RH knee disc, around x 44-48,
  rows 114-120 (f0, f1). The still there shows background below the belly.
- ne: dark body fill hangs to the right of the far hind foot (LH), around x 64-75, rows 148-160 (seen on f1-f3; check
  all 8). A 1 px enclosed gap on f1. (Corrected 2026-11-05: I first listed a dark vertical line in the LH foot pad
  as a defect. Touch-up found it sits between the grey pad and the lilac toe in the approved still too, so it is part
  of the design and stays.)
- s: 11 px gaps between a front foot and the jaw on f0, f1, f4 and f5 are real space. Leave them open.
Sway (agreed with touch-up, Preston judges): lean R = -1 0 +1 +1 +1 0 -1 -1 for f0-f7 (character's right +). Screen x:
-R in s and se, +R in n and ne, 0 in e. Bob 0 1 1 0 0 1 1 0 on top of the dip. Lock each planted foot by its own box
(se RH stands on row 135, ne RF on row 136, far above the bottom rows).
After Preston approves touch-up's version, the walk lane packs it with tools/art/pack_hippo_3d.py and a backup. Check
the hippo kit's full_speed_walk_rate (stride 0.48 m/s), or its feet will slide in game.
FILE walk\fennec_s\run1\brief.txt
You are making pixel-art walk frames for a game. Write only inside D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1.
Read anything under D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba. Do not touch any other folder, assets, art or tools, and do not
commit. Run tag: fennec-trot35-run1.

The machine: the violet S robot fennec fox. Its standing stills at 35 degrees are in
D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\views_stand35 (s, se, e, ne, n; 160x160, feet row 140, 15 colours, raw
with no outline; README.txt there says how they were made). The same stills placed on the game's 192x192 canvas (at +16,
+20, so the lowest foot is on row 160) are in run1\stills; work on those. Preston, the game's owner, approved this
standing pose; above the legs the walk must match it exactly.
Read first: D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\WALK_PLAYBOOK.md (sections 1, 5, 7, 9 and 11) and the green gorilla's
35-degree walk, the method that worked last: D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run1 (brief.txt, REPORT.txt,
build.py, verify.py, rig.json), ape_l\run2 (brief.txt, REPORT.txt, walk\build_frontback.py) and ape_l\final\README.txt.
Reuse that method and its scripts rather than starting again. What went wrong there: run 1's ne far limb came out cut
off and broken, and in e, se and ne a limb sliding back left a transparent slit inside the body that had to be filled
afterwards. The fennec's old trot at the low camera shows how its legs and tail moved:
D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\fennec_s\take-b\anim\codex-set-a\walk (motion only; its camera and outline are wrong).

Keep run1\REPORT.txt current: write it once the first view is drawn and update it after each view, so a report exists even
if the run is stopped by its token cap.

Job, run 1: an 8-frame trot, 100 ms a frame (10 fps), looping, in these views: e, se, ne. The gait is a trot in
diagonal pairs: the right front foot (RF) and left hind foot (LH) swing f5-f7; the left front foot (LF) and right hind
foot (RH) swing f1-f3; both pairs are planted on f0 and f4. Each foot swings for 3 frames with lifts of 2, 3 and 2 px.
Each foot's path (offset along the body in side-view px, + = forward, and lift) is in run1\targets.json "paths"; per view
the path turns into screen px as offset * f * u, with the lift straight up:
  e: u = (1, 0), f = 1.0; a planted foot moves (-3.50, +0.00) px a frame on the canvas (x, y; y down)
  se: u = (0.8674, 0.4975), f = 0.8152; a planted foot moves (-2.47, -1.42) px a frame on the canvas
  ne: u = (0.8674, -0.4975), f = 0.8152; a planted foot moves (-2.47, +1.42) px a frame on the canvas
The step is 3.5 px in e (the game's speed), so targets fall on half pixels: round each frame's position so a planted
foot's moves alternate (3 and 4 px in e) and its total stays within 0.5 px of the target. targets.json "views" gives the
sole centre of every labelled foot in each still for every frame; run1\guide_<view>.png shows them (the still faded, then
a bar per foot per frame, filled when planted and outlined when lifted, with a tick for the lift). R and L are the
fennec's own right and left; its right side is the near side in e, se and ne. The labels are in
D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\feet.json: check them and say if one is wrong. Feet partly or wholly
hidden in the still: e LH and LF (they peek out at x 90-94 and 119-121, row 153), se LH, ne LF. Draw a hidden foot only
where the new pose shows it.

Method: no image tool, native pixels, as the gorilla walk did it. The body, head, ears, eyes, chest plate and tail are the
still, pixel for pixel, dipping 1 px on f0 and f4 (the frames where a pair lands). The tail is the still's tail moved
whole: it dips 1 px one frame later than the body (f1 and f5), never redrawn, never curled, and never drawn over a leg.
The legs are the still's own legs re-posed. Each leg is hip, upper leg, round steel knee disc, lower leg and steel foot.
The foot and lower leg move together to the target, the knee disc moves about half as far, and the upper leg between hip
and knee is redrawn as a leaning band in the still's own colours and shading bands, stepped 1 px per row or two, with its
lit edge and its dark seam kept. On a lifted foot the lower leg rises with it. Every part is the still's own pixels
TRANSLATED, never rotated or resampled (rotation left dark specks and slivers on the rhino). Every joint and every join
with the body (shoulder, hip, knee) is redrawn by hand: no seams, no straight cut edges, no gaps, no part ending abruptly.
Where a leg slides back, draw the body behind it: no slit may show the background inside the body. Keep each leg's length
within 1 px of the still's. The far legs (darker in the still) are built the same way, one shade darker on their own
ramp, behind the near legs and the body.

Pass marks (report each):
- Foot lock: for each foot, its sole centre on its k-th planted frame (k = 0 to 4 in order, wrapping past f7) minus k
  times the planted step above stays within 2 px in x and y. Measure from the frame pixels, not from your targets. Write
  a footprint picture per view. Also run these and report their results:
  python D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\tools\walk_tools.py footlock
    --targets D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1\targets.json
    --frames D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1\walk\frames --views e se ne
    --still-dir D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1\stills
    --out D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1\footlock.json
  python D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\tools\walk_tools.py seams
    --targets D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1\targets.json
    --frames D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1\walk\frames --views e se ne
    --still-dir D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1\stills --body e0c8ff a46bff 6a3cc8 2e1a6e
  For a foot hidden behind the body in a frame, measure it from your leg layer and say so. Seams must report no slits.
- Each visible foot meets its target within 1 px; lifts exact.
- Above the legs every frame equals the still (after the 1 px dip; the tail one frame later): report the % of differing
  pixels per frame.
- 192x192 transparent frames, the stills' 15 colours only, binary alpha, no holes, no stray pixels, nothing touching the
  canvas edge. A planted foot nearer the camera than in the still may sit up to 3 px below row 160 (perspective); report
  each frame's lowest row.
- Judge boards at 4x with the game's 1 px dark sel-out added (the packer adds it; use the selout function in
  D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\touchup\touchup_machine.py), with the still beside them, then a 5x zoom of the legs
  of every frame: look at every knee, hip and foot.

Output in run1: walk\frames\<view>_f0.png to <view>_f7.png for e, se and ne (raw, no outline, like the stills), a preview
gif per view (100 ms, loop), footprint pictures, board.png (per view: the still, then your 8 frames, with sel-out, at
4x), legs_zoom.png, checks.txt, footlock.json, and REPORT.txt (under 30 lines): tokens if known, the foot names you used,
the foot-lock spreads, target errors, the seams result, upper-body match, lowest rows, and by eye: does each view read as
this fennec trotting forward in diagonal pairs, legs like the still, whole and clean, tail straight and clear of the legs?

FILE walk\fennec_s\run1\build.py
"""fennec-trot35-run1: hand-traced native parts; gorilla integer-translation method.
