R=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
# Reuse the walk lane's single-pass seam detector and body-band colour choice.
_seamfile=R.parents[2]/'walk/tools/walk_tools.py'
_tree=ast.parse(_seamfile.read_text());_ns={'np':np}
exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in _tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in ['find_slits','fill_colour']],type_ignores=[]),str(_seamfile),'exec'),_ns)
find_slits,fill_colour=_ns['find_slits'],_ns['fill_colour']

P={h:tuple(bytes.fromhex(h))+(255,) for h in '141218 211e27 2e1a6e 3d3a44 6a3cc8 9d9ba2 a46bff d6d6dc e0c8ff f0d27a fff4d6'.split()}

# Each knee housing includes its complete steel disc. Each lower block includes
# the unchanged native shin, ankle and steel foot, with sole contact endpoints.
D={
'e':{
 'RH':dict(knee=[(84,133),(89,133),(96,136),(98,139),(97,144),(93,146),(81,146),(80,140),(81,137)],lower=[(81,147),(90,147),(91,153),(94,154),(94,158),(92,160),(81,160)],root=(87,131),k=(88,140),join=(86,147),sole=(86.5,160)),
 'RF':dict(knee=[(110,131),(114,131),(117,134),(117,139),(115,143),(108,143),(108,135)],lower=[(107,144),(116,144),(116,152),(119,154),(119,159),(107,159),(107,153)],root=(112,129),k=(112,137),join=(112,144),sole=(112.5,159)),
 },
'se':{
 'RH':dict(knee=[(81,127),(85,127),(90,130),(92,134),(91,137),(88,140),(80,141),(79,136),(79,131)],lower=[(80,141),(87,141),(87,146),(91,147),(91,151),(88,153),(80,153),(79,151),(79,147)],root=(84,125),k=(84,133),join=(84,141),sole=(84,153)),
 'RF':dict(knee=[(99,133),(103,133),(108,136),(110,139),(108,143),(104,145),(98,144),(96,140),(97,136)],lower=[(97,145),(106,145),(106,153),(109,154),(109,159),(107,160),(98,160),(97,157)],root=(102,132),k=(102,139),join=(102,145),sole=(103,160)),
 'LF':dict(knee=[(112,139),(116,139),(119,142),(119,147),(111,148),(110,144)],lower=[(111,148),(118,148),(118,150),(121,151),(121,154),(118,156),(109,156),(109,151)],root=(114,137),k=(114,143),join=(114,148),sole=(114,156)),
 },
'ne':{
 'RH':dict(knee=[(91,135),(96,135),(102,137),(103,140),(102,143),(100,146),(96,148),(90,147),(89,141),(89,137)],lower=[(90,148),(97,148),(97,152),(101,154),(101,158),(98,160),(90,160)],root=(94,133),k=(95,141),join=(94,148),sole=(94.5,160)),
 'RF':dict(knee=[(108,124),(112,124),(115,127),(115,131),(112,133),(107,132),(106,128)],lower=[(107,133),(113,133),(113,140),(116,142),(116,146),(114,148),(105,148),(103,145),(103,142),(107,140)],root=(110,122),k=(110,128),join=(110,133),sole=(109.5,148)),
 'LH':dict(knee=[(80,137),(85,137),(87,141),(87,146),(78,147),(78,141)],lower=[(78,147),(85,147),(85,149),(89,149),(89,152),(86,155),(78,155),(77,152),(77,149)],root=(83,135),k=(82,141),join=(82,147),sole=(82,155)),

TAIL={
'e':[(50,120),(82,120),(82,133),(80,139),(50,141)],
'se':[(60,110),(84,110),(84,122),(78,128),(60,128)],
'ne':[(60,120),(84,120),(84,127),(90,126),(94,129),(92,132),(86,138),(76,150),(60,150)]}
# Remove all visible far-leg peeks from the pinned body, in addition to near parts.
PEEKS={
'e':[(91,147,98,154),(117,145,122,154)],

def rects(boxes):
 m=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
 for x0,y0,x1,y1 in boxes:m[y0:y1+1,x0:x1+1]=True
 return m

def dark(a):
 a=a.copy()
 # simultaneous palette mapping
 old=a.copy()
 for c,d in [('e0c8ff','a46bff'),('a46bff','6a3cc8'),('6a3cc8','2e1a6e'),('d6d6dc','9d9ba2'),('9d9ba2','3d3a44')]:a[np.all(old==P[c],axis=2)]=P[d]
 return a

def sources(v):
 ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');used=np.zeros((192,192),bool);parts={}
 chest=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
 # Preserve the entire cream chest plate, including its steel highlights and rim.
 if v=='se':
  for yy,lo,hi in [(133,106,117),(134,105,117),(135,105,117),(136,105,117),(137,105,117),(138,106,116),(139,106,116),(140,106,115),(141,107,114),(142,106,113),(143,106,112),(144,107,110),(145,108,110)]:chest[yy,lo:hi+1]=True
 yellow=np.logical_or.reduce([np.all(ref==P[h],axis=2) for h in ['f0d27a','fff4d6']]);chest|=yellow&(X>100)
 native_tail=b.mask(TAIL[v])&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
 used|=native_tail

 for n in ['RF','RH','LF','LH']:
  if n not in D[v]:continue
  q=D[v][n];km=b.mask(q['knee'])&~used&~chest&(ref[:,:,3]>0);lm=b.mask(q['lower'])&~used&~km&~chest&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
  parts[n]={'knee':b.piece(ref,km),'lower':b.piece(ref,lm)};used|=km|lm
 # Hidden legs reconstructed with the same translated native parts, one ramp darker.
 # Their still peeks set depth/foot position. No pasted fragment can become detached.
 hidden={'e':[('LH','RH',6,-7),('LF','RF',8,-6)],'se':[('LH','RH',10,-5)],'ne':[('LF','RF',-12,-5)]}[v]
 for n,fromn,dx,dy in hidden:
  q=dict(D[v][fromn]);q['root']=tuple(np.array(q['root'])+[dx,dy]);q['k']=tuple(np.array(q['k'])+[dx,dy]);q['join']=tuple(np.array(q['join'])+[dx,dy]);q['sole']=tuple(np.array(q['sole'])+[dx,dy]);q['hidden']=True;D[v][n]=q
  parts[n]={p:dark(b.shift(a,dx,dy)) for p,a in parts[fromn].items()}
 if v=='ne':
  # The far hind knee is occluded by the tail in the still, not a tail plate.
  parts['LH']['knee']=dark(b.shift(parts['RH']['knee'],-12,-5))
  D[v]['LH']['k']=(83,136);D[v]['LH']['root']=(83,132)
 # Use the actual visible far sole where there is enough source to recover it.
 if v=='se':
  p=b.piece(ref,rects([(91,142,96,148)]));parts['LH']['lower']=p;D[v]['LH']['sole']=(94,148);D[v]['LH']['join']=(94,142)
 tail=b.piece(ref,native_tail)
 used|=tail[:,:,3]>0;used|=rects(PEEKS[v])&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
 body=b.piece(ref,~used);b.comp(body,b.piece(ref,chest))
 # Remaining native shank outlines are limb paint, not pinned trunk paint.
 # Removing them here prevents the old stationary vertical slivers seen on ape.
 if v=='e':body[(Y>=143)&(X>=100)&(X<=123)]=0
 if v=='se':body[(Y>=146)&(X>=90)&(X<=123)]=0
 if v=='ne':
  body[Y>=147]=0;body[(Y>=133)&(X>=103)&(X<=118)]=0;body[(Y>=139)&(X>=89)&(X<=102)]=0

 # Finish the flank behind the native hip housings. Only body above the leg
 # attachment is filled; the space between shin/foot pairs remains background.
 # Nearest native body band copied by row, as in the gorilla's seam repair.
 fills={'e':[(82,132,96,136),(108,131,117,142)],'se':[(81,127,89,130),(98,133,105,136)],'ne':[(90,135,99,138),(107,124,113,132)]}[v]
 for x0,y0,x1,y1 in fills:
  for y in range(y0,y1+1):
   for x in range(x0,x1+1):
    if body[y,x,3]==0 and ref[y,x,3]:body[y,x]=P['2e1a6e']
 return ref,parts,body,tail

def band(a,p0,p1,width=6,far=False):
 # Native palette bands, stepped by <=1px per row wherever room permits.
 x0,y0=p0;x1,y1=p1;y0,y1=int(y0),int(y1)
 if y1<y0:x0,y0,x1,y1=x1,y1,x0,y0
 for y in range(y0,y1+1):
  t=(y-y0)/max(1,y1-y0);cx=int(np.floor(x0+(x1-x0)*t+.5));l=cx-width//2;r=l+width
  a[y,l:r]=P['2e1a6e'];a[y,l+1:r-1]=P['6a3cc8' if not far else '2e1a6e']
  a[y,l+1:l+3]=P['a46bff' if not far else '6a3cc8'];a[y,r-1]=P['141218']

def make(v):
 for d in ['walk/frames','walk/layers','source']:(R/d).mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 ref,parts,body,tail=sources(v)
 for label,a in [('body',body),('tail',tail)]:Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'source/{v}_{label}.png')
 for n,pr in parts.items():
  for p,a in pr.items():Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
 frames=[];rig=[]
 for f in range(8):
  dip=int(f in (0,4));layers={}
  for n,pr in parts.items():
   q=D[v][n];sole=np.array(q['sole'],float);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f];vt=T['views'][v]
   ideal=np.array(vt['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre']) if n in vt['visible_feet'] else sole+off*np.array(vt['u'])*vt['f']-[0,lift]
   dx,dy=np.floor(ideal-sole+.5).astype(int);kx=int(np.floor(dx*.5+.5));ky=int(np.floor((dy+dip)*.5+.5))
   a=np.zeros_like(ref);root=np.array(q['root'])+[0,dip];knee=np.array(q['k'])+[kx,ky];join=np.array(q['join'])+[dx,dy]
   band(a,root,knee,width=7 if n.endswith('H') else 6,far=n.startswith('L'))
   # The shin and foot remain one rigid native translation. Draw the small
   # articulated connection UNDER both translated pieces so no butt seam shows.
   band(a,knee,join,width=6,far=n.startswith('L'))
   b.comp(a,b.shift(pr['lower'],dx,dy));b.comp(a,b.shift(pr['knee'],kx,ky))
   bottom=int(sole[1]+dy);a[Y>bottom]=0
   raw=b.shift(pr['lower'],dx,dy);a[(Y>=bottom-1)&(raw[:,:,3]==0)]=0
   layers[n]=a;Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png')
   rig.append(dict(foot=n,frame=f,delta=[int(dx),int(dy)],knee_delta=[kx,ky],source_sole=sole.tolist(),target=ideal.tolist(),lift=lift,hidden_in_still=n not in vt['visible_feet']))
  Image.fromarray(b.shift(tail,0,int(f in (1,5)))).save(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_tail_f{f}.png')
  a=np.zeros_like(ref)
  # Tail always behind legs. Pinned trunk hides far legs only where it really exists.
  b.comp(a,b.shift(tail,0,int(f in (1,5))))
  for n in ['LF','LH']:b.comp(a,layers[n])
  b.comp(a,b.shift(body,0,dip))
  for n in ['RF','RH']:b.comp(a,layers[n])
  if v=='se':
   chestmask=b.mask([(106,133),(117,133),(117,137),(115,141),(110,145),(107,143),(105,138)])
   b.comp(a,b.shift(b.piece(ref,chestmask),0,dip))
  # Above the attachments, pin both paint and transparent background exactly.
  cut={'e':131,'se':127,'ne':124}[v]+dip
  pinned=b.shift(ref,0,dip);tt=b.shift(tail,0,dip);pinned[tt[:,:,3]>0]=0;b.comp(pinned,b.shift(tail,0,int(f in (1,5))))
  a[:cut]=pinned[:cut]
  # Fill only actual enclosed holes: translated native body paint where available,
  # deepest violet in mechanical socket recesses. No blanket silhouette expansion.
  holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0)
  st=b.shift(ref,0,dip)
  for yy,xx in zip(*np.where(holes)):
   c=tuple(st[yy,xx]);a[yy,xx]=c if c in [P[h] for h in ['e0c8ff','a46bff','6a3cc8','2e1a6e']] else P['2e1a6e']
  # One pass only, as required by WALK_PLAYBOOK section 9. All filled
  # pixels are saved for review; no iterative expansion toward an idle silhouette.
  sl=find_slits(a,ref,dip);done=set();pre=a.copy()
  for xx,yy in sorted(sl,key=lambda p:(p[1],p[0])):
   c=fill_colour(a,ref,dip,xx,yy,done,{P[h][:3] for h in ['e0c8ff','a46bff','6a3cc8','2e1a6e']})
   a[yy,xx]=tuple(c)+(255,) if tuple(c) in {P[h][:3] for h in ['e0c8ff','a46bff','6a3cc8','2e1a6e']} else P['2e1a6e'];done.add((xx,yy))
  holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0);a[holes]=P['2e1a6e']
  Image.fromarray(pre).save(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_before_seams_f{f}.png')
  Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'walk/frames/{v}_f{f}.png');frames.append(a)
 old=json.loads((R/'rig.json').read_text()) if (R/'rig.json').exists() else {};old[v]=rig;(R/'rig.json').write_text(json.dumps(old,indent=2))
 boards(v,ref,frames)
 report=R/'REPORT.txt';s=report.read_text() if report.exists() else 'fennec-trot35-run1. Tokens: final total unavailable.\nRF/RH near right; LF/LH far left; supplied labels retained.\n'
 s+=f'{v}: eight native frames drawn; measurements and visual review pending.\n';report.write_text(s)
 print(v,'drawn')

def boards(v,ref,frames):
 sel=b.load_selout();font=ImageFont.truetype('C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf',18);bg=(74,80,76,255)
 board=Image.new('RGBA',(768*9,800),bg);zoom=Image.new('RGBA',(420*9,250),bg);review=Image.new('RGBA',(360*5,400*2),bg)
 for j,a in enumerate([ref]+frames):
  tile=Image.fromarray(sel(a)[0]);board.alpha_composite(tile.resize((768,768),Image.Resampling.NEAREST),(j*768,30));ImageDraw.Draw(board).text((j*768+8,5),v+' '+('STILL' if not j else f'f{j-1}'),font=font,fill='white')
  zoom.alpha_composite(tile.crop((60,118,144,162)).resize((420,220),Image.Resampling.NEAREST),(j*420,25));ImageDraw.Draw(zoom).text((j*420+8,3),v+' '+('STILL' if not j else f'f{j-1}'),font=font,fill='white')
  col,row=(0,0) if j==0 else ((j-1)%4+1,(j-1)//4)
  review.alpha_composite(tile.crop((50,85,140,180)).resize((360,380),Image.Resampling.NEAREST),(col*360,row*400+20));ImageDraw.Draw(review).text((col*360+5,row*400+1),v+' '+('STILL' if not j else f'f{j-1}'),font=font,fill='white')
 board.convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_board.png');zoom.convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_legs_zoom.png');review.convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_review.png')
 for half in range(2):zoom.crop((420*(1+half*4),0,420*(5+half*4),250)).convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_legs_half{half}.png')
 pics=[]
 for a in frames:
  tile=Image.new('RGBA',(768,768),bg);tile.alpha_composite(Image.fromarray(sel(a)[0]).resize((768,768),Image.Resampling.NEAREST));pics.append(tile.convert('RGB'))
 pics[0].save(R/f'{v}_preview.gif',save_all=True,append_images=pics[1:],duration=100,loop=0,disposal=2,optimize=False)
 views=[vv for vv in ['e','se','ne'] if (R/f'{vv}_board.png').exists()]
 out=Image.new('RGB',(768*9,800*len(views)),bg[:3]);zz=Image.new('RGB',(420*9,250*len(views)),bg[:3])
 for i,vv in enumerate(views):out.paste(Image.open(R/f'{vv}_board.png'),(0,800*i));zz.paste(Image.open(R/f'{vv}_legs_zoom.png'),(0,250*i))
 out.save(R/'board.png');zz.save(R/'legs_zoom.png')

if __name__=='__main__':
 for v in sys.argv[1:] or ['e','se','ne']:make(v)

You are making pixel-art walk frames for a game. Write only inside D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run2.
Read anything under D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba. Do not touch any other folder (run1 included), assets, art or
tools, and do not commit. Run tag: fennec-trot35-run2.

This is run 2 of the violet S fennec's trot at 35 degrees. Run 1 (D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\fennec_s\run1)
made the e, se and ne views. Read run1\brief.txt (the machine, the gait, the method, the rules and the pass marks all
still apply), run1\REPORT.txt, run1\LANE_REVIEW.txt (the walk lane's review), and run1\build.py, rig.json, checks.txt and

D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\WALK_PLAYBOOK.md section 7 for the front and back views, and the gorilla's run 2
(D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run2: brief.txt, REPORT.txt, walk\build_frontback.py), which did the same.
The stills on the 192 canvas are in run2\stills; the targets (the same plan as run 1, with s and n included) are in
run2\targets.json, and the guides are run2\guide_<view>.png. Preston approved folding two repairs of run 1 into this run.

Work in this order, and keep run2\REPORT.txt up to date after each part, so a report exists even if the run is stopped:

Part 1, repair ne (all 8 frames). Preston saw run 1's ne: the far hind leg's dark upper band is drawn over the tail, so
the tail's light top band breaks into dark blocks, with dark specks around the tail root and between the hind legs. A 5x
zoom is run1\lane_defects_zoom.png (top two rows: the still, then f0-f7). In ne the tail is nearer the camera than the far
hind leg and above it, so draw in this order: far legs (LH, LF), then the tail, then the body, then the near legs (RH,
RF). The tail is the still's own tail moved whole (1 px dip on f1 and f5), its light top band and cream tip complete in
every frame; nothing of the far hind leg may cover it; the far hind leg shows only below the tail, as in the still.
Remove every loose dark speck. Keep everything else in run 1's ne frames as it is, and keep every foot's lock.
Pass mark: in the box x 58-91, y 128-149 the tail's light violet pixels (e0c8ff and a46bff) number at least 63 in every
frame (the still has 70; run 1 had 11-34), counted after the tail's dip. Write run2\walk\frames\ne_f0.png to ne_f7.png.

Part 2, repair e (check all 8 frames, fix f3-f6 and any other). In run 1's e the gold chest plate at the near front
shoulder breaks into loose gold fragments as the front leg moves (bottom row of run1\lane_defects_zoom.png: the still,
then f3-f6). The chest plate is part of the pinned body: one solid gold shape with its highlight, base and shadow bands
and its dark seam, behind the near front leg's top. Where the leg uncovers it, draw the plate whole, continuing its bands
and seam; where the leg covers it, draw the leg's edge cleanly over it. No gold pixel may sit loose or in specks.
Pass mark: every e frame has the still's number of separate gold pieces (cream-gold fff4d6, f0d27a, b8923a, 8-connected:
the still has 4) and no gold piece of 4 px or fewer. Write all 8 frames, unchanged ones copied from run 1, to
run2\walk\frames\e_f0.png to e_f7.png. se needs no repair.

Part 3, s. Part 4, n. Each is an 8-frame trot loop, 100 ms a frame, with run 1's gait: RF and LH swing f5-f7, LF and RH
swing f1-f3, both pairs planted on f0 and f4, the body dips 1 px on f0 and f4, the tail one frame later (f1 and f5). Per
view, the path turns into screen px as offset * f * u, with the lift straight up:
  s: u = (0, 1), f = 0.5736; a planted foot moves (0.00, -2.01) px a frame on the canvas (x, y; y down)
  n: u = (0, -1), f = 0.5736; a planted foot moves (0.00, +2.01) px a frame on the canvas
At 35 degrees a step toward or away from the camera shows at 0.57 of its length, so these views need real foot travel up
and down the screen, not reordered side frames. In s the front feet are the near feet (row 160) and the hind feet are far
(row 149), mostly hidden beside the front legs; in n the hind feet are near (row 160) and the front feet far (row 140),
peeking out at the sides. A foot nearer the camera than in the still may sit up to 4 px below row 160. Draw order: in s,
hind legs, then body, then front legs; in n, front legs, then body, then hind legs, then the tail, which points at the
camera from the top of the rump and is drawn over the inner edges of the hind legs only where the still shows it so. As
the hind legs move, no tail colour may be left on a leg and no leg colour on the tail (the old walk left gold tail pixels
on the inner hind leg). In both views each leg moves straight up and down the screen with its knee disc, joints redrawn
by hand; the legs stay apart from each other.

Pass marks, for ne, e, s and n: as in run1\brief.txt, including seams with no slits inside the body. Run these and report
their results:

from pathlib import Path
import ast
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageFont
from collections import deque
R=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
B=R.parents[2]
def fill_holes(op):
    seen = op.copy()
    queue = deque()
    for x in range(192):
        for y in (0, 191):
            if not seen[y, x]:
                seen[y, x] = True
                queue.append((y, x))
    for y in range(192):
        for x in (0, 191):
            if not seen[y, x]:
                seen[y, x] = True
                queue.append((y, x))
    while queue:
        y, x = queue.popleft()
        for yy, xx in ((y - 1, x), (y + 1, x), (y, x - 1), (y, x + 1)):
            if 0 <= yy < 192 and 0 <= xx < 192 and (not seen[yy, xx]):
                seen[yy, xx] = True
                queue.append((yy, xx))
    return ~seen | op

def mask(poly):
    im = Image.new('1', (192, 192))
    ImageDraw.Draw(im).polygon(poly, fill=1)
    return np.array(im)

def shift(a, dx=0, dy=0):
    b = np.zeros_like(a)
    ys, xs = np.where(a[:, :, 3] > 0)
    xx = xs + int(dx)
    yy = ys + int(dy)
    ok = (xx >= 0) & (xx < 192) & (yy >= 0) & (yy < 192)
    b[yy[ok], xx[ok]] = a[ys[ok], xs[ok]]
    return b

def comp(a, b):
    m = b[:, :, 3] > 0
    a[m] = b[m]

def piece(a, m):
    b = a.copy()
    b[~m] = 0
    return b

def support(a):
    yy, xx = np.where(a[:, :, 3] > 0)
    row = int(yy.max())
    xs = np.where(a[row - 1:row + 1, :, 3].any(axis=0))[0]
    return np.array([(xs.min() + xs.max()) / 2, row], float)

def load_selout():
    sp = B / 'rhino_l/take-a/anim/selout_rhino.py'
    tree = ast.parse(sp.read_text())
    ns = {'np': np, 'OUTLINE': np.array([20, 26, 22], np.uint8)}
    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == 'selout'], type_ignores=[]), str(sp), 'exec'), ns)
    return ns['selout']

def pieces(op):
    seen = np.zeros_like(op)
    out = []
    for y, x in zip(*np.where(op)):
        if seen[y, x]:
            continue
        todo = [(int(y), int(x))]
        seen[y, x] = True
        pts = []
        while todo:
            yy, xx = todo.pop()
            pts.append((yy, xx))
            for dy in (-1, 0, 1):
                for dx in (-1, 0, 1):
                    ny, nx = (yy + dy, xx + dx)
                    if 0 <= ny < 192 and 0 <= nx < 192 and op[ny, nx] and (not seen[ny, nx]):
                        seen[ny, nx] = True
                        todo.append((ny, nx))
        out.append(pts)
    return sorted(out, key=len, reverse=True)

def locate(a, src):
    sole = b.support(src)
    ys, xs = np.where((src[:, :, 3] > 0) & (b.Y >= sole[1] - 5))
    cols = src[ys, xs]
    best = None
    for dy in range(-12, 13):
        for dx in range(-14, 15):
            score = float(np.all(a[ys + dy, xs + dx] == cols, axis=1).mean())
            key = (score, -abs(dx) - abs(dy))
            if best is None or key > best[0]:
                best = (key, dx, dy)
    (_, score_unused), dx, dy = best
    score = best[0][0]
    sy = int(sole[1])
    by, bx = np.where((src[:, :, 3] > 0) & (b.Y >= sy - 1))
    left, right = (int(bx.min()), int(bx.max()))
    ends = []
    for x in [left, right]:
        ends.append(any((np.array_equal(a[y + dy, x + dx], src[y, x]) for y in [sy - 1, sy] if src[y, x, 3] > 0)))
    return (sole + [dx, dy], score, [dx, dy], all(ends))
Y,X=np.indices((192,192))

b=sys.modules[__name__]

"""hippo-walk35-run2: run1 native parts, fennec run2 depth ordering.
All writes are relative to this run2 directory. Run with python -B.
"""
import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode=True
from pathlib import Path
import json, importlib.util
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageFont
R=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(R.parent/'run1'))
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('run1_build',R.parent/'run1/build.py')
old=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(old)
b=old.b;P=old.P;X=old.X;Y=old.Y;load=old.load
T=json.loads((R/'targets.json').read_text())
save=lambda a,p:Image.fromarray(a).save(R/p)
def rect(x0,y0,x1,y1):return (X>=x0)&(X<=x1)&(Y>=y0)&(Y<=y1)

def sources(v):
    ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');parts={};used=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
    if v=='s':
        # Native jaw contour, traced at 8x. This foreground piece is NEVER
        # assigned to either foot or knee, including its charcoal underside.
        jaw=np.zeros_like(used)
        left={134:71,135:71,136:71,137:70,138:70,139:70,140:70,141:70,142:70,143:70,144:70,145:70,146:71,147:71,148:71,149:71,150:71,151:72,152:73,153:74,154:75,155:76,156:77}
        for y,l in left.items():jaw[y,l:192-l]=True
        for n,x0,x1 in [('RF',50,85),('LF',106,141)]:
            knee=rect(x0,138,x1,149)&~jaw
            lower=rect(x0,150,x1,160)&~jaw
            # No background and no jaw paint enters the moving sources.
            parts[n]={'knee':b.piece(ref,knee),'lower':b.piece(ref,lower)}
            used|=knee|lower
        # The main shoulder housings (above row138) remain pinned. Front
        # upper links emerge beneath them, beside the tusks and jaw.
        defs={'RF':dict(root=(65,134),k=(65,145),join=(66,151),w=5),
              'LF':dict(root=(126,134),k=(126,145),join=(125,151),w=5)}
        cutoff=111
    else:
        # Rear discs end at row140. The steel joint, dark casing and whole
        # toe/heel assembly beneath them move; no leg-shaped body patches.
        for n,x0,x1 in [('LH',58,86),('RH',105,133)]:
            knee=rect(x0,141,x1,150)
            lower=rect(x0,151,x1,160)
            parts[n]={'knee':b.piece(ref,knee),'lower':b.piece(ref,lower)}
            used|=knee|lower
        defs={'LH':dict(root=(71,139),k=(71,146),join=(72,152),w=6),
              'RH':dict(root=(120,139),k=(120,146),join=(119,152),w=6)}
        cutoff=128
    pinned=b.piece(ref,~used)
    # Separate the trunk from its fixed foreground assemblies. In s the
    # head and jaw conceal the trunk; in n the rump/tail lies over the legs.
    body=b.piece(pinned,Y<=cutoff)
    foreground=b.piece(pinned,Y>cutoff)
    # Hidden native feet are translated dark-ramp counterparts. Their entire
    # layers are kept for measurement; depth compositing reveals only pixels
    # which actually clear the original head/body/near legs.
    pairs=[('RH','RF'),('LH','LF')] if v=='s' else [('LF','LH'),('RF','RH')]
    for n,src in pairs:
        dy=-25 if v=='s' else -28
        parts[n]={p:old.dark(b.shift(a,0,dy)) for p,a in parts[src].items()}
        defs[n]={k:(tuple(np.array(z)+[0,dy]) if k in ['root','k','join'] else z) for k,z in defs[src].items()}
    for n,pp in parts.items():
        defs[n]['sole']=b.support(pp['lower']).tolist()
        for p,a in pp.items():save(a,f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
    save(body,f'source/{v}_body.png');save(foreground,f'source/{v}_foreground.png')
    # Discs/head/rump stay complete in the foreground; this also documents
    # the exact fixed pixels separately from the trunk belly audit.
    save(pinned,f'source/{v}_pinned.png')
    return ref,parts,body,foreground,defs

def make(v):
    for d in ['source','walk/frames','walk/layers']:(R/d).mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    ref,parts,body,foreground,D=sources(v);frames=[];rig=[]
    for f in range(8):
        dip=int(f in [0,4]);ll={}
        for n,pp in parts.items():
            q=D[n];sole=np.array(q['sole']);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f];vt=T['views'][v]
            target=np.array(vt['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre']) if n in vt['visible_feet'] else sole+off*np.array(vt['u'])*vt['f']-[0,lift]
            delta=np.floor(target+[0,lift]-sole+.5).astype(int)-[0,lift]
            # Depth only: the native pad centre can differ half a pixel from
            # a label; never shift a leg sideways to compensate for that.
            delta[0]=0
            dx,dy=map(int,delta);kd=np.array([0,int(np.floor((dy+dip)*.5+.5))])
            socket=np.array(q['root'])+[0,dip]
            # The attachment slides inside the pinned housing in this depth
            # projection. Keep the two-link projected length native while the
            # exposed knee moves half the foot travel. The socket-to-root
            # connector is concealed by the housing, apart from its seam.
            root=np.array(q['root'])+delta;kn=np.array(q['k'])+kd;jn=np.array(q['join'])+delta
            native_length=np.linalg.norm(np.array(q['k'])-q['root'])+np.linalg.norm(np.array(q['join'])-q['k'])
            length_error=float(abs(np.linalg.norm(kn-root)+np.linalg.norm(jn-kn)-native_length))
            far=n.endswith('H') if v=='s' else n.endswith('F')
            a=np.zeros_like(ref)
            old.casing(a,socket,root,q['w']+3,far);old.casing(a,root,kn,q['w']+3,far);old.casing(a,kn,jn,q['w']+3,far)
            old.band(a,socket,root,q['w'],far);old.band(a,root,kn,q['w'],far);old.band(a,kn,jn,q['w'],far)
            # Continue the native casing's full bevel/seam profile across a
            # one- or two-row downward extension. A narrow centre band alone
            # would leave a horizontal cut in the wide inner casing of n.
            ky,kx=np.where(pp['knee'][:,:,3]>0);ly,lx=np.where(pp['lower'][:,:,3]>0)
            boundary=int(ky.max());top=int(ly.min())
            cols=np.where(pp['knee'][boundary,:,3]>0)[0]
            for row in range(boundary+int(kd[1])+1,top+dy):
                a[row,cols]=pp['knee'][boundary,cols]
            b.comp(a,b.shift(pp['knee'],*kd));raw=b.shift(pp['lower'],dx,dy);b.comp(a,raw)
            bottom=int(sole[1]+dy);a[Y>bottom]=0;a[(Y>=bottom-1)&(raw[:,:,3]==0)]=0
            ll[n]=a;save(a,f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png')
            rig.append(dict(foot=n,frame=f,delta=delta.tolist(),knee_delta=kd.tolist(),source_sole=sole.tolist(),target=target.tolist(),lift=lift,root=root.tolist(),socket=socket.tolist(),knee=kn.tolist(),ankle=jn.tolist(),native_projected_length=float(native_length),leg_length_error=length_error,hidden_in_still=n not in vt['visible_feet']))
        a=np.zeros_like(ref);far=['RH','LH'] if v=='s' else ['LF','RF'];near=['RF','LF'] if v=='s' else ['LH','RH']
        for n in far:b.comp(a,ll[n])
        b.comp(a,b.shift(body,0,dip))
        # Housing foreground is drawn before near legs, then its actual
        # fixed pixels are restored; gaps in the native outline remain open.
        b.comp(a,b.shift(foreground,0,dip))
        for n in near:b.comp(a,ll[n])
        b.comp(a,b.shift(foreground,0,dip))
        st=b.shift(ref,0,dip);cut=(138 if v=='s' else 141)+dip
        a[:cut]=st[:cut]
        # Only enclosed join holes; no automatic slit fill in open leg gaps.
        holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0)
        for y,x in zip(*np.where(holes)):a[y,x]=st[y,x] if st[y,x,3] else P['211e27']
        protected=b.shift(body,0,dip);b.comp(protected,b.shift(foreground,0,dip))
        for n in near:
            rr=next(q for q in rig if q['frame']==f and q['foot']==n)
            b.comp(protected,b.shift(parts[n]['lower'],*rr['delta']))
        # Cleanup is limited to redrawn connectors; every fixed pixel and
        # every exposed native pad pixel is locked against recolouring.
        b.comp(protected,b.shift(foreground,0,dip))
        edits=old.joint_cleanup(a,ref,b.shift(protected,0,-dip),parts,[q for q in rig if q['frame']==f],dip)
        save(a,f'walk/frames/{v}_f{f}.png');save(b.shift(body,0,dip),f'walk/layers/{v}_body_f{f}.png');save(b.shift(foreground,0,dip),f'walk/layers/{v}_foreground_f{f}.png')
        (R/f'walk/layers/{v}_join_edits_f{f}.json').write_text(json.dumps(edits))
        frames.append(a)
    path=R/'rig.json';allrig=json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else {};allrig[v]=rig;path.write_text(json.dumps(allrig,indent=2))
    report=R/'REPORT.txt';lines=report.read_text().splitlines() if report.exists() else ['hippo-walk35-run2; tokens: unavailable.','Reused run1 native translations/joints and fennec run2 front/back depth order.']
    lines=[s for s in lines if not s.startswith(v+':')];lines.append(v+': 8 native frames and source/layers saved; audit pending.');report.write_text('\n'.join(lines)+'\n')
    old.R=R;old.boards(v,ref,frames)
    print(v,'source soles',{n:q['sole'] for n,q in D.items()})
    return frames

def combine():
    for suffix in ['board.png','legs_zoom.png']:
        ims=[Image.open(R/f'{v}_{suffix}').convert('RGB') for v in ['s','n']]
        out=Image.new('RGB',(max(i.width for i in ims),sum(i.height for i in ims)));y=0
        for im in ims:out.paste(im,(0,y));y+=im.height
        out.save(R/suffix)
if __name__=='__main__':
    for v in sys.argv[1:] or ['s','n']:make(v)
    if all((R/f'{v}_board.png').exists() for v in ['s','n']):combine()
"""Independent colour-stamp audit, reusing ape_l/run1/verify.py searches."""
import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode=True
import json,hashlib,ast
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageFont
import build as m
b=m.b;R=m.R;T=m.T;load=m.load;X,Y=m.X,m.Y
_tree=ast.parse((R.parents[2]/'walk/tools/walk_tools.py').read_text());ns={'np':np}
exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in _tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='find_slits'],type_ignores=[]),'walk_tools.py','exec'),ns)
find_slits=ns['find_slits']
def specks(a,details=False):
 out=[]
 for c in set(map(tuple,a[a[:,:,3]>0])):
  mask=np.all(a==c,axis=2)&(Y>=120)&(Y<=164)
  for cc in b.pieces(mask):
   if len(cc)<=2:out.append((bytes(c[:3]).hex(),cc))
 return out if details else len(out)

def audit():

 'Reused ape_l/run1 native.py locate(): exhaustive sole colour-stamp translations, no rig/target used in search.',

    if not ok:fails.append(f'{v} f{f} {key}')
   marked=a.copy()

  tile=Image.fromarray(ref).crop((35,95,151,165)).resize((580,350),Image.Resampling.NEAREST);base=Image.new('RGBA',tile.size,(65,73,80));base.alpha_composite(tile);seamboard.paste(base.convert('RGB'),(0,25));ds.text((8,3),v+' still',font=font,fill='white');seamboard.save(R/f'{v}_seams_review.png')
  footpic=Image.new('RGB',(1480,4*270),(58,66,62));dr=ImageDraw.Draw(footpic)
  for j,n in enumerate(['RF','LH','LF','RH']):

    pp.append(p);tracks.append(tr);lines.append('CONTACT '+v+' '+n+' '+json.dumps(tr))
    if err.max()>1 or actual_lift!=lift or score!=1 or not ends:fails.append(f'{v} {n} f{f} contact')
    if padmatch!=1 or kneematch!=1 or rr['leg_length_error']>1 or rr['upper_link_error'] is None:fails.append(f'{v} {n} f{f} native parts/length')
   start=(T['gait'][n]+3)%8;order=[(start+k)%8 for k in range(5)];step=np.array(T['views'][v]['planted_step_px']);locked=np.array([pp[f]-k*step for k,f in enumerate(order)]);spread=np.ptp(locked,axis=0)

   oy=j*270;dr.text((10,oy+4),f'{v} {n}: planted {order}; pixel-measured spread {spread[0]:.3f}/{spread[1]:.3f}px',font=font,fill='white');colors=['#ffb575','#75e5cf','#e99fe2','#c9e978','#aab8ff'];orig=locked.mean(0)

  footpic.save(R/f'{v}_footprints.png');lines.append('SUMMARY '+v+' '+json.dumps({n:{k:z for k,z in q.items() if k!='frames'} for n,q in vr['feet'].items()}))

 for v in ['e','se','ne']:

"""Rhino cam35 touch-up (sprite touch-up lane, Claude, 2026-11-04).

Preston: "Make a session for touching up sprites using a synthesis of all we've learned all our tools etc.
Especially aseprite. Sel-out, Cap ink, and all the other professional terms or tasks they would use."

Input  (read only): ../views_cam35/{s,se,e,ne,n}.png   (approved by Preston, "Thats it looks good")
Ref    (read only): ../views/{...}.png                  (the original approved stills, the craft standard)
Output:             ../views_cam35_touchup/{...}.png    raw, no outline: the packer adds sel-out at pack time
                    ./board.png, ./metrics.json, ./views_selout_light/ (the light-following sel-out option)

The rule: recolour only. Alpha never changes, so canvas, feet row, offsets and silhouette stay exact.
Passes, in order (each counted separately in metrics.json):
  1. specks   cluster cleanup: yellow clusters of 1-2 px take the colour most of their neighbours have
  2. seams    capped ink on lit faces: a yellow pixel darker than both neighbours across it, with both
              neighbours lit (base or highlight), in a line of 3+ (one-pixel breaks closed), becomes the
              darkest colour #141218: a panel line between plates
  2b. rims    bevel rim: highlight kept on a RIM-deep band along each big highlight plate's lit (top left)
              edges, base yellow inside; ends the "whole plate filled with highlight" look
  3. gaps     charcoal frame: a shadow or deep yellow pixel with a dark line (accent or charcoal, 6+ px) on
              its left or above it sits in that plate's cast shadow (light from the top left) and becomes
              charcoal shadow; capped at CAP x the approved view's frame + seam share
Eyes and the ring around them are never touched.
"""

'se':[(89,121),(95,120),(101,124),(102,129),(107,134),(111,136),(123,133),(128,126),(130,119),(132,114),(137,112),(141,116),(144,122),(144,128),(148,133),(149,144),(145,152),(138,155),(130,152),(123,151),(118,157),(112,161),(101,161),(94,158),(91,151),(93,143),(92,139),(90,135)],
'ne':[(130,88),(140,90),(147,94),(150,99),(151,105),(147,111),(143,110),(144,106),(144,101),(139,98),(133,99),(130,95)]}
# Torso outline behind moving limb masks. No fill outside this contour.
TORSO={
'e':[(69,117),(91,104),(116,109),(132,127),(135,143),(127,146),(113,148),(102,150),(90,150),(78,147),(68,143)],
'se':[(60,83),(74,80),(100,91),(126,109),(133,129),(123,141),(104,147),(91,143),(80,135),(73,124),(68,115),(63,106),(59,96)],
'ne':[(83,90),(107,80),(133,94),(141,111),(135,120),(123,130),(112,138),(102,140),(91,138),(79,125),(75,108)]}
# Entire far set lies under the shell; offsets estimated from the still's body depth.
FAR={'e':(0,-20),'se':(24,-17),'ne':(-22,-19)}
def sources(v):
 ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');parts={};used=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
 cm=b.mask(CLAW[v])&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
 # Do not assign near red foot/band pixels to a large claw bounding polygon.
 if v=='e':cm &= ~rect(110,145,130,161)
 if v=='se':cm &= ~rect(72,141,91,154)
 fg=b.piece(ref,cm)
 for n,q in D[v].items():
  m=b.mask(q['poly'])&~cm&~used&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
  # Native complete outer dark/red contour at the soles.
  part=b.piece(ref,m);foot=b.piece(part,Y>=q['ft']);parts[n]={'native':part,'foot':foot}
  used|=m;q['sole']=b.support(foot).tolist()
  xx=np.where(m)[1];q['span']=[int(xx.min()),int(xx.max())]
 # Remove static leg shadows below each source part as well.
 body=b.piece(ref,~used&~cm)
 torso=b.mask(TORSO[v]);gun=[P[h] for h in ['26262e','34343e','555562','7e7e8e']]
 fill=used&torso
 for yy,xx in zip(*np.where(fill)):
  # Continue the native row's nearest unobscured body shade, with darkest
  # body recess used immediately under ball joints; never extend the belly.
  choices=[(abs(x-xx),x) for x in range(max(0,xx-20),min(192,xx+21)) if body[yy,x,3] and tuple(body[yy,x]) in gun and torso[yy,x]]
  body[yy,xx]=body[yy,min(choices)[1]] if choices else P['26262e']
 for n,pp in parts.items():
  for p,a in pp.items():save(a,f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
 # Clone native far legs, darker ramp, as in hippo/fennec. The shell occludes them.
 dx,dy=FAR[v]
 for r in ['R3','R2','R1']:
  n='L'+r[1];parts[n]={p:b.shift(a,dx,dy) for p,a in parts[r].items()}
  for p,a in parts[n].items():
   old=a.copy()
   for h,j in [('eeeef2','b8b8c2'),('b8b8c2','9a9aa6'),('9a9aa6','7e7e8e'),('7e7e8e','555562'),('f85a4a','ee2c26'),('ee2c26','c41216'),('c41216','6a0a10')]:a[np.all(old==P[h],axis=2)]=P[j]
  q=dict(D[v][r]);q['root']=(np.array(q['root'])+[dx,dy]).tolist();q['sole']=(np.array(q['sole'])+[dx,dy]).tolist();q['top']+=dy;q['band']=tuple(y+dy for y in q['band']);q['ft']+=dy;D[v][n]=q
  for p,a in parts[n].items():save(a,f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
 save(body,f'source/{v}_body.png');save(fg,f'source/{v}_claws.png');save(b.piece(ref,fill),f'source/{v}_body_reconstruction_area.png')
 return ref,parts,body,fg

def rowband(dst,src,ya,yb,na,nb,xa,xb):
 # Re-draw native bevel/shade row profiles on an integer staircase. No pixel
 # rotation, filtering or resampling; the native foot is handled separately.
 for yy in range(int(na),int(nb)+1):
  t=(yy-na)/max(1,nb-na);sy=int(np.floor(ya+(yb-ya)*t+.5));dx=int(np.floor(xa+(xb-xa)*t+.5))
  xs=np.where(src[sy,:,3]>0)[0];xs=xs[(xs+dx>=0)&(xs+dx<192)]
  if 0<=yy<192:dst[yy,xs+dx]=src[sy,xs]

def limb(pp,q,delta,dip):
 dx,dy=map(int,delta);a=np.zeros_like(pp['native']);top=q['top'];ba,bb=q['band'];ft=q['ft'];kdy=int(np.floor((dy+dip)*.48+.5));kdx=int(np.floor(dx*.5+.5))
 # A fixed ball covers the uppermost join. Knee band and lit edge remain native.
 rowband(a,pp['native'],top,ba-1,top+dip,ba+kdy-1,0,kdx)
 rowband(a,pp['native'],ba,bb,ba+kdy,bb+kdy,kdx,kdx)
 rowband(a,pp['native'],bb+1,ft-1,bb+kdy+1,ft+dy-1,kdx,dx)
 b.comp(a,b.shift(pp['foot'],dx,dy))
 # Measure projected two-link reach between ball, knee and ankle; retained in rig.
 root=np.array(q['root'])+[0,dip];k0=np.array([q['sole'][0],(ba+bb)/2]);ank0=np.array([q['sole'][0],ft]);kn=k0+[kdx,kdy];ank=ank0+[dx,dy]
 length0=np.linalg.norm(k0-np.array(q['root']))+np.linalg.norm(ank0-k0);length=np.linalg.norm(kn-root)+np.linalg.norm(ank-kn)
 return a,[kdx,kdy],float(length-length0)

def boards(v,ref,frames):
 sel=b.load_selout();font=ImageFont.truetype('C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf',18);bg=(62,71,67,255)
 board=Image.new('RGBA',(768*9,798),bg)
 box={'e':(64,123,162,164),'se':(46,94,151,164),'ne':(88,95,153,148)}[v];w=(box[2]-box[0])*5;h=(box[3]-box[1])*5
 zoom=Image.new('RGBA',(w*9,h+30),bg)
 for j,a in enumerate([ref]+frames):
  tile=Image.fromarray(sel(a)[0]);board.alpha_composite(tile.resize((768,768),Image.Resampling.NEAREST),(j*768,30));ImageDraw.Draw(board).text((j*768+8,5),v+' '+('STILL' if j==0 else f'f{j-1}'),font=font,fill='white')
  zoom.alpha_composite(tile.crop(box).resize((w,h),Image.Resampling.NEAREST),(j*w,30));ImageDraw.Draw(zoom).text((j*w+8,5),v+' '+('STILL' if j==0 else f'f{j-1}'),font=font,fill='white')
 board.convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_board.png');zoom.convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_legs_zoom.png')
 for j in range(3):zoom.crop((j*3*w,0,(j+1)*3*w,h+30)).convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_legs_part{j}.png')
 pics=[]
 for a in frames:
  im=Image.new('RGBA',(768,768),bg);im.alpha_composite(Image.fromarray(sel(a)[0]).resize((768,768),Image.Resampling.NEAREST));pics.append(im.convert('RGB'))
 # GIF ticks are 10ms, so alternating120/130ms expresses the requested 1s cycle.
 pics[0].save(R/f'{v}_preview.gif',save_all=True,append_images=pics[1:],duration=[120,130]*4,loop=0,disposal=2,optimize=False)
 rgba=[Image.fromarray(a) for a in frames];rgba[0].save(R/f'{v}_preview.png',save_all=True,append_images=rgba[1:],duration=125,loop=0,disposal=0,blend=0)
 for suffix in ['board.png','legs_zoom.png']:
  ims=[Image.open(R/f'{vv}_{suffix}').convert('RGB') for vv in ['e','se','ne'] if (R/f'{vv}_{suffix}').exists()];out=Image.new('RGB',(max(i.width for i in ims),sum(i.height for i in ims)),bg[:3]);yy=0
  for im in ims:out.paste(im,(0,yy));yy+=im.height
  out.save(R/suffix)

def make(v):
 for d in ['source','walk/frames','walk/layers']:(R/d).mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 ref,parts,body,fg=sources(v);frames=[];rig=[];vt=T['views'][v]
 for f in range(8):
  dip=int(f in [0,4]);ll={}
  for n,pp in parts.items():
   q=D[v][n];sole=np.array(q['sole']);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f];target=sole+off*np.array(vt['u'])*vt['f']-[0,lift]
   # Native source sole may be half a pixel from supplied guide centre.
   if n in vt['visible_feet']:target=np.array(vt['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre'])
   delta=np.floor(target+[0,lift]-sole+.5).astype(int)-[0,lift]
   a,kd,le=limb(pp,q,delta,dip);ll[n]=a;save(a,f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png')
   rig.append(dict(foot=n,frame=f,delta=delta.tolist(),knee_delta=kd,source_sole=sole.tolist(),target=target.tolist(),lift=lift,projected_length_change=le,hidden_in_still=n.startswith('L')))
  a=np.zeros_like(ref)
  for n in ['L3','L2','L1']:b.comp(a,ll[n])
  pin=b.shift(body,0,dip);front=b.shift(fg,0,dip);b.comp(a,pin)
  for n in ['R3','R2','R1']:b.comp(a,ll[n])
  b.comp(a,front)
  # Full canvas above the first moving row is the approved image, including alpha.
  cut=min(q['top'] for n,q in D[v].items() if n.startswith('R'))+dip;a[:cut]=b.shift(ref,0,dip)[:cut]
  # Fill only enclosed join holes, preserving open interleg negative space.
  holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0)
  for yy,xx in zip(*np.where(holes)):a[yy,xx]=pin[yy,xx] if pin[yy,xx,3] else P['26262e']
  save(a,f'walk/frames/{v}_f{f}.png');save(pin,f'walk/layers/{v}_body_f{f}.png');save(front,f'walk/layers/{v}_claws_f{f}.png');frames.append(a)
 path=R/'rig.json';old=json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else {};old[v]=rig;path.write_text(json.dumps(old,indent=2))
 path=R/'REPORT.txt';lines=path.read_text().splitlines() if path.exists() else ['lobster-walk35-run1; tokens unavailable.','Native hippo/fennec method reused; R1/front, R2/middle, R3/rear near right. Labels retained.','Tripod A L1/R2/L3 planted f0-3; B R1/L2/R3 planted f4-7; body/claws/tail dip f0/f4.']
 lines=[s for s in lines if not s.startswith(v+':')];lines.append(v+': eight native frames and source/layers drawn; pixel audit/visual review pending.');path.write_text('\\n'.join(lines)+'\\n')
 boards(v,ref,frames);print(v,'drawn', {n:q['sole'] for n,q in D[v].items()})
if __name__=='__main__':
 for v in sys.argv[1:] or ['e','se','ne']:make(v)

def cmd_footlock(a):
    t = json.load(open(a.targets))
    report, ok_all = {}, True
    still_cols = None
    if a.still_dir:
        still_cols = {}
    for view in (a.views or list(t['views'])):
        vt = t['views'][view]
        frames = [load_rgba(os.path.join(a.frames, a.pattern.format(view=view, i=i))) for i in range(8)]
        alphas = [f[:, :, 3] > 0 for f in frames]
        step = vt['planted_step_px']
        vr = {'feet': {}, 'frames': []}
        print(f'== {view}: planted step {step}')
        for foot, fd in vt['visible_feet'].items():
            w = fd['still_sole'][1] - fd['still_sole'][0] + 1
            meas, err = [], 0.0
            for fr in fd['frames']:
                m = measure_sole(alphas[fr['frame']], fr['sole_centre'][0], fr['sole_centre'][1], w)
                meas.append(m)
                if m:
                    err = max(err, abs(m[0] - fr['sole_centre'][0]), abs(m[1] - fr['sole_centre'][1]))
            planted = [fr['lift'] == 0 for fr in fd['frames']]
            start = next(i for i in range(8) if planted[i] and not planted[(i - 1) % 8])
            run = []
            i = start
            while planted[i] and len(run) < 8:
                run.append(i)
                i = (i + 1) % 8
            corr = [(meas[i][0] - j * step[0], meas[i][1] - j * step[1]) for j, i in enumerate(run) if meas[i]]
            sx = max(c[0] for c in corr) - min(c[0] for c in corr) if corr else None
            sy = max(c[1] for c in corr) - min(c[1] for c in corr) if corr else None
            ok = corr and len(corr) == len(run) and sx <= 2 and sy <= 2
            ok_all &= bool(ok)
            vr['feet'][foot] = {'planted_frames': run, 'spread_px': [sx, sy], 'max_target_error_px': round(err, 2),
                                'measured': meas, 'pass': bool(ok)}
            print(f'   {foot}: planted f{run}, lock spread x {sx:.2f} y {sy:.2f} px, '
                  f'max off target {err:.2f} px -> {"pass" if ok else "CHECK"}')
        still = None
        if a.still_dir:
            still = load_rgba(os.path.join(a.still_dir, f'{view}.png'), a.still_offset, frames[0].shape[0])
            sc = still[still[:, :, 3] > 0][:, :3]
            still_set = {tuple(c) for c in sc}
        for i, (fr, al) in enumerate(zip(frames, alphas)):
            alpha_raw = fr[:, :, 3]
            lowest = int(np.nonzero(al.any(axis=1))[0].max())
            semi = int(((alpha_raw > 0) & (alpha_raw < 255)).sum())
            edge = bool(al[0].any() or al[-1].any() or al[:, 0].any() or al[:, -1].any())
            pieces = count_pieces(al)
            row = {'frame': i, 'lowest_row': lowest, 'semi_alpha_px': semi, 'canvas_edge': edge,
                   'pieces': len(pieces), 'stray_px': sum(pieces[1:])}
            if still is not None:
                fc = fr[al][:, :3]
                row['off_palette_px'] = int(sum(1 for c in map(tuple, fc) if c not in still_set))
            vr['frames'].append(row)
        print('   frames: ' + '; '.join(
            f"f{r['frame']} low {r['lowest_row']} pieces {r['pieces']}"
            + (f" stray {r['stray_px']}" if r['stray_px'] else '')
            + (f" semi {r['semi_alpha_px']}" if r['semi_alpha_px'] else '')
            + (' EDGE' if r['canvas_edge'] else '')
            + (f" offpal {r['off_palette_px']}" if r.get('off_palette_px') else '') for r in vr['frames']))
        report[view] = vr
    if a.out:
        json.dump(report, open(a.out, 'w'), indent=1)
    print('ALL FEET LOCKED' if ok_all else 'SOME FEET NEED A LOOK')


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- seams
BODY_GREENS = {(0x22, 0x30, 0x1d), (0x47, 0x69, 0x3a), (0x6c, 0x9b, 0x4a), (0x9c, 0xcb, 0x62)}  # gorilla body bands


def find_slits(fr, st, dip, n=6):
    """Pixels solid in the idle still (after the dip) but now transparent and enclosed within n px on all four sides:
    the gap a limb leaves when it slides back from a pinned body (gorilla run 1 and 2, 2026-11-05)."""
    a = fr[:, :, 3] > 0
    s = np.zeros_like(a)
    s[dip:] = st[:st.shape[0] - dip, :, 3] > 0
    out = []
    for y, x in zip(*np.nonzero(~a & s)):
        if a[y, max(0, x - n):x].any() and a[y, x + 1:x + 1 + n].any() and a[max(0, y - n):y, x].any() \
                and a[y + 1:y + 1 + n, x].any():
            out.append((int(x), int(y)))
    return out


def fill_colour(fr, st, dip, x, y, done, body):
    """The band on both sides if they agree, else the still's own body colour there, else the next body colour right."""
    row = fr[y]
    op = row[:, 3] > 0
    left = next((tuple(row[i, :3]) for i in range(x - 1, -1, -1) if op[i] or (i, y) in done), None)
    right = next((tuple(row[i, :3]) for i in range(x + 1, row.shape[0]) if op[i]), None)
    if left == right and left in body:
        return left
    sc = tuple(st[y - dip, x, :3]) if st[y - dip, x, 3] else None
    if sc in body:
        return sc
    for i in range(x + 1, row.shape[0]):
        if op[i] and tuple(row[i, :3]) in body:
            return tuple(row[i, :3])
    return right or left


def cmd_seams(a):
    t = json.load(open(a.targets))
    body = {tuple(bytes.fromhex(c)) for c in a.body} if a.body else BODY_GREENS
    if a.fix:
        os.makedirs(a.fix, exist_ok=True)
    for view in (a.views or list(t['views'])):
        st = load_rgba(os.path.join(a.still_dir, f'{view}.png'))
        counts = []
        for i in range(8):
            name = a.pattern.format(view=view, i=i)
            fr = load_rgba(os.path.join(a.frames, name))
            dip = 1 if i in t['dip_frames'] else 0
            sl = find_slits(fr, st, dip)
            counts.append(len(sl))
            if a.fix:
                done = set()
                for x, y in sorted(sl, key=lambda q: (q[1], q[0])):
                    fr[y, x, :3] = fill_colour(fr, st, dip, x, y, done, body)
                    fr[y, x, 3] = 255
                    done.add((x, y))
                Image.fromarray(fr).save(os.path.join(a.fix, name))
        print(f'{view}: enclosed slit px per frame {counts}' + (' (filled)' if a.fix else ''))
    print('Look at every slit before filling: a real gap between a limb and the body must stay open.')


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- mirror
def cmd_mirror(a):
    gait = GAITS[a.gait]
    swing = len(GAIT_OPTS.get(a.gait, {}).get('lifts', [2, 3, 2]))
    up = [sorted(f for f, s in gait.items() if (i - s) % 8 < swing) for i in range(8)]
    print(f'gait {a.gait}: feet up per frame in the drawn views (s, se, e, ne, n)')
    print('frame | drawn up | mirrored view, same frame (physical) | mirrored view at frame+4 (physical) | same?')
    same4 = True
    for i in range(8):
        m0 = sorted(swap_lr(f) for f in up[i])
        m4 = sorted(swap_lr(f) for f in up[(i + 4) % 8])
        same4 &= (m4 == up[i])
        print(f'  f{i}  | {",".join(up[i]) or "-":8} | {",".join(m0) or "-":36} | {",".join(m4) or "-":36} | '
              f'{"yes" if m4 == up[i] else "no"}')
    print('A turn from s to sw (or n to nw) keeps the frame index today, so the up foot swaps sides in one frame.')
    print('Offset +4 keeps the same physical foot up: ' + ('yes, for every frame' if same4 else 'NO, check the gait'))

def locate(a,src):

import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode=True
import json,ast
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageFont
from scipy.ndimage import label
import build as m
R=m.R;b=m.b;T=m.T;X=m.X;Y=m.Y;load=m.load

def specks(a):
    total=0
    for c in set(map(tuple,a[a[:,:,3]>0])):
        lab,num=label(np.all(a[120:165]==c,axis=2),np.ones((3,3)))
        sizes=np.bincount(lab.ravel())[1:];total+=int(((sizes>0)&(sizes<=2)).sum())
    return total
